================================================================================
                    DOCLOQ DOCUMENT MANAGEMENT SYSTEM
                        FULL DOCUMENTATION
================================================================================

Project      : DocLoq - Secure Document Management System
Type         : Cybersecurity Capstone Project
Stack        : React 19 + Express 5 + PostgreSQL 16 + Drizzle ORM
AI           : OpenAI (per-subsystem models, see §7 / §62) + Qdrant + MongoDB Cache
Security     : AES-256-GCM + Vault Transit + ClamAV + Honeytokens + Blockchain
Storage      : Cloudflare R2 (S3-compatible) + HashiCorp Vault (HA-ready)
Signing      : DocuSeal e-Signature
Editor       : OnlyOffice Document Server
Auth         : JWT + TOTP 2FA + Email OTP + Cloudflare Turnstile (CAPTCHA)

================================================================================
1. SYSTEM ARCHITECTURE OVERVIEW
================================================================================

DocLoq is a security-first document management system designed for
enterprise/government environments that require maximum data protection.

Architecture Layers:
--------------------

  [Frontend - React 19 + Vite + TailwindCSS]
       |
       | HTTPS + JWT Auth
       v
  [Backend - Express 5 + Node.js]
       |
       +---> [PostgreSQL 16]     Primary database (Drizzle ORM)
       +---> [Cloudflare R2]      S3-compatible cloud object storage
       +---> [Vault HA]          Key management (Transit engine, Raft HA)
       +---> [ClamAV]            Malware scanning
       +---> [Qdrant Cloud]      Vector database (AI semantic search)
       +---> [MongoDB]           AI analysis result cache
       +---> [OpenAI API]        GPT-4.1-mini + text-embedding-3-small
       +---> [OnlyOffice]        Document editing/conversion
       +---> [DocuSeal]          E-signature service
       +---> [Polygon Chain]     Blockchain anchoring (ethers.js)

Multi-Tenant Design:
- Every user belongs to an organization
- All data is scoped by organizationId
- Separate encryption keys per document (envelope encryption)

================================================================================
2. INFRASTRUCTURE & DOCKER SERVICES
================================================================================

Development: backend/docker-compose.yml
Production:  backend/docker-compose.prod.yml (overlay)

+------------------+---------------------+----------+---------------------------+
| Service          | Image               | Port     | Purpose                   |
+------------------+---------------------+----------+---------------------------+
| db               | postgres:16-alpine  | 5436     | Primary database          |
| redis            | redis:7-alpine      | 6379     | Rate limiter store        |
| vault            | hashicorp/vault:1.17| 8200     | Key management (Transit)  |
| clamav           | clamav/clamav:stable| 3310     | Antivirus/malware scanner |
| qdrant           | qdrant/qdrant:v1.13.1| 6333,   | Vector DB (AI search)     |
|                  |                     | 6334     |                           |
| mongodb          | mongo:7.0           | 27017    | AI analysis cache         |
+------------------+---------------------+----------+---------------------------+

Production overlay (docker-compose.prod.yml) adds:
+------------------+---------------------+----------+---------------------------+
| vault-1          | hashicorp/vault:1.17| 8200     | Vault HA leader (Raft)    |
| vault-2          | hashicorp/vault:1.17| -        | Vault HA follower         |
| vault-3          | hashicorp/vault:1.17| -        | Vault HA follower         |
| cloudflared      | cloudflare/         | -        | Cloudflare Tunnel ingress |
|                  | cloudflared:latest  |          |                           |
+------------------+---------------------+----------+---------------------------+

  NOTE (2026-06-30 audit): the Docker Swarm VPS stack (infra/docker-stack.yml, §18/§23)
  additionally runs onlyoffice/documentserver, docuseal/docuseal, and gitea/gitea
  (+ its own postgres:16-alpine) behind Traefik — these are NOT in the dev compose above.

Object Storage: Cloudflare R2 (cloud-hosted, S3-compatible)
  - docloq-documents   : Encrypted document storage
  - docloq-archive     : Archived document versions
  - docloq-qrcodes     : QR code verification images

  Note: R2 replaces the previous MinIO self-hosted setup. The S3 API
  client (@aws-sdk/client-s3) works identically with R2. Storage
  provider is set via STORAGE_PROVIDER=r2 in .env.

Docker Volumes (dev):
  - postgres_data, clamav_data, qdrant_data, mongodb_data

Docker Volumes (prod, adds):
  - vault_data_1, vault_data_2, vault_data_3

Network: docloq_network (bridge)

3. ENVIRONMENT VARIABLES
================================================================================

File: backend/.env

--- Core ---
PORT                    Server port (default: 3000)
NODE_ENV                Environment: development | production
FRONTEND_URL            Frontend URL for CORS

--- Database ---
DATABASE_URL            PostgreSQL connection string

--- Rate Limiter (Redis) ---
REDIS_URL               Redis connection URL (redis://redis:6379 in Docker)

--- Authentication ---
JWT_SECRET              JWT signing secret
TURNSTILE_SECRET_KEY    Cloudflare Turnstile secret key (siteverify)
VITE_TURNSTILE_SITE_KEY Cloudflare Turnstile site key (frontend build-arg)
HCAPTCHA_ENABLED        [DEPRECATED 2026-05-24] Set to false. Replaced by Turnstile
HCAPTCHA_SECRET         [DEPRECATED 2026-05-24] Not read by code anymore

--- OnlyOffice ---
BACKEND_URL             Backend URL (for OnlyOffice callbacks)
BACKEND_URL_DOCKER      Backend URL from Docker network
ONLYOFFICE_URL          OnlyOffice Document Server URL

--- Encryption ---
ENCRYPTION_MASTER_KEY   AES-256 master key (64 hex chars)
QR_SIGNING_SECRET       HMAC-SHA256 signing key for QR codes
QR_VERIFICATION_BASE_URL  QR verification endpoint

--- Malware Scanner ---
SCANNER_ENABLED         Enable ClamAV scanning (true/false)
CLAMAV_HOST             ClamAV host
CLAMAV_PORT             ClamAV port (3310)

--- Storage (Cloudflare R2 / S3-compatible) ---
STORAGE_PROVIDER        Storage backend: r2 | minio | local
S3_ENDPOINT             S3 endpoint URL (R2: https://<ACCOUNT_ID>.r2.cloudflarestorage.com)
S3_ACCESS_KEY           S3/R2 access key ID
S3_SECRET_KEY           S3/R2 secret access key
S3_DOCUMENTS_BUCKET     Document storage bucket (docloq-documents)
S3_ARCHIVE_BUCKET       Archive bucket (docloq-archive)
S3_QR_BUCKET            QR code images bucket (docloq-qrcodes)
S3_FORCE_PATH_STYLE     Path-style addressing (true for R2 and MinIO)
S3_REGION               S3 region (R2 uses "auto")

--- Cloudflare (Production) ---
CLOUDFLARE_TUNNEL_TOKEN Cloudflare Tunnel token (from Zero Trust dashboard)
CLOUDFLARE_ACCOUNT_ID   Cloudflare account ID
TRUST_PROXY             Enable trust proxy for Cloudflare (true in production)

--- Key Management (Vault) ---
KEY_PROVIDER            Key provider: vault | local
VAULT_ADDR              Vault server address
VAULT_TOKEN             Vault auth token
VAULT_TRANSIT_KEY       Transit engine key name

--- Vector Database (Qdrant) ---
QDRANT_URL              Qdrant server/cloud URL
QDRANT_API_KEY          Qdrant Cloud API key
QDRANT_COLLECTION       Collection name

--- AI Analysis & Chatbot ---
AI_ANALYSIS_MODEL       Doc-analysis model (default: gpt-4.1-mini)
CHATBOT_MODEL           DoKi chatbot model (separate from AI_ANALYSIS_MODEL)
CHATBOT_RATE_LIMIT_PER_MINUTE   Per-user chatbot rate limit
OPENAI_API_KEY          OpenAI API key

--- AI Projects quotas (read by ai-project services) ---
AI_PROJECT_MAX_PER_ORG     Max AI projects per organization
AI_PROJECT_MAX_SOURCES     Max sources per project
AI_PROJECT_URL_MAX_BYTES   Max bytes fetched per URL source
AI_PROJECT_CHAT_LIMIT_24H  Per-project chat messages per 24h
AI_IMAGE_MODEL             Studio image-output model (default: gpt-image-1). Output PNGs
                           are encrypted under the project DEK and stored in R2, and are
                           crypto-shreddable like documents (§62)

--- MongoDB (AI Cache) ---
MONGO_URI               MongoDB connection URI (the ONLY mongo var read by backend
                        code; MONGO_USER/MONGO_PASSWORD below are for the mongo
                        container init, not read by the app)
MONGO_USER              MongoDB username (container init only)
MONGO_PASSWORD          MongoDB password (container init only)

--- Upload Limits ---
MAX_FILE_SIZE_MB        Maximum upload size (default: 50)

--- Email (SMTP) ---
SMTP_HOST               SMTP server host
SMTP_PORT               SMTP port (587)
SMTP_USER               SMTP username
SMTP_PASS               SMTP password
SMTP_FROM               From email address
SMTP_FROM_NAME          From display name (default 'DocLoq')
                        ALIAS NOTE (verified in email.service.js): each SMTP_* var has a
                        MAIL_* fallback read second — MAIL_HOST/MAIL_PORT/MAIL_USERNAME/
                        MAIL_PASSWORD/MAIL_FROM_ADDRESS/MAIL_FROM_NAME. SMTP_* wins when both set.
APP_URL                 Public app base URL used to build links inside emails (falls back
                        to FRONTEND_URL, then https://docloq.site) (§63)
EMAIL_HELP_URL          Override for the "Help Center" email footer link (default APP_URL/contact)
EMAIL_PRIVACY_URL       "Privacy" email footer link — rendered ONLY if set (no dead links) (§63)
EMAIL_TERMS_URL         "Terms" email footer link — rendered ONLY if set (§63)

--- Auth password recovery / rate limits (§63) ---
AUTH_FORGOT_LIMIT           Max /auth/forgot-password requests per IP per window (default 5)
AUTH_RESET_LIMIT            Max /auth/reset-password requests per IP per window (default 10)
AUTH_CHANGE_PASSWORD_LIMIT  Max /auth/change-password requests per IP per window (default 10)
AUTH_RECOVERY_WINDOW_MIN    Rate-limit window in minutes for the three above (default 15)

--- OnlyOffice / Editor (read by code) ---
ONLYOFFICE_URL_INTERNAL Internal OnlyOffice Document Server URL (server-to-server)
ONLYOFFICE_SECRET       JWT secret for OnlyOffice signed tokens (per-process if unset)

--- CAPTCHA — reCAPTCHA (LEGACY, see note) ---
RECAPTCHA_SECRET_KEY    reCAPTCHA secret. NOTE: recaptcha.service.js exists but is NOT
RECAPTCHA_MIN_SCORE     imported anywhere — the active CAPTCHA is Cloudflare Turnstile.
                        These vars are read only inside that unwired legacy service.

--- Misc toggles / internal (read by code) ---
DOWNLOAD_WATERMARK_ENABLED  Per-download invisible watermark toggle (§15)
ALLOW_LOCAL_KEY_FALLBACK    Permit local key provider fallback when Vault unavailable
ADMIN_GATE_SECRET           Gate secret for the /kicawkicaw super-admin entrypoint (§44)
BLOCKCHAIN_CONFIRMATIONS    Block confirmations awaited for blockchain anchors, default 2
                            (§16). NOTE: the var is BLOCKCHAIN_CONFIRMATIONS, not
                            POLYGON_CONFIRMATIONS (other blockchain vars use POLYGON_* prefix).
OCR_LANG / OCR_RENDER_DPI / OCR_MIN_CONFIDENCE / OCR_ENABLE_OSD / OCR_TESSDATA_PATH
                            OCR pipeline tuning (§32)
VITE_TURNSTILE_SITE_KEY     Frontend build-arg (also referenced server-side for parity)

--- OSINT Document Tracking (§49) ---
OSINT_TRACKING_ENABLED  Master switch for visible-code stamping (default ON; set to
                        'false' to disable). Read by upload.config.js tracking.enabled.
OSINT_AUTOSCAN_ENABLED  Opt-in daily discovery cron (default OFF). When 'true', scans
                        every tracked doc's canary code via Google CSE every 24h.
GOOGLE_CSE_KEY          Google Custom Search JSON API key (required for OSINT scan)
GOOGLE_CSE_CX           Google Custom Search engine ID (cx)
                        NOTE: these four are read by code but are NOT yet present in
                        backend/.env.example — add them manually when enabling OSINT.

================================================================================
4. DATABASE SCHEMA (62 TABLES)
================================================================================

ORM: Drizzle ORM with PostgreSQL

--- ENUMS ---

user_role:            owner, admin, user   (pgEnum 'user_role' — §42 refactor; was 6 pre-2026-06-20)
permission_type:      read, read_edit, full_access
document_status:      active, archived, revoked, expired, deleted
verification_status:  pending, verified, failed, revoked, expired
task_status:          pending, in_progress, completed, cancelled
task_priority:        low, medium, high, urgent
audit_action:         create, read, update, delete, download, share,
                      verify, restore, archive
share_type:           view_only, can_edit
archive_status:       active, glacier, deep_archive, deleted
form_instance_status: draft, active, completed, cancelled
workflow_action:      fill, review, approve, sign
workflow_step_status: pending, in_progress, completed, skipped
admin_role:           super_admin, admin, support

--- ORGANIZATIONS & STRUCTURE ---

1. organizations
   - id (UUID PK), name, slug (UNIQUE)
   - subscriptionTier, hasBlockchainFeature
   - awsRegion, s3Bucket, kmsKeyArn
   - dataRetentionDays (default 2555), archiveAfterDays (default 365)
   - gdprCompliant, pdpCompliant
   - settings (JSONB), isActive, createdAt, updatedAt

2. departments
   - id (UUID PK), organizationId (FK), name, description
   - color, createdBy, isActive, createdAt, updatedAt

3. teams
   - id (UUID PK), organizationId (FK), name, description
   - color, createdBy, isActive, createdAt, updatedAt

4. teamMembers
   - id (UUID PK), teamId (FK), userId (FK)
   - isTeamLead, addedBy, createdAt

--- USERS & AUTHENTICATION ---

5. users
   - id (UUID PK), organizationId (FK), email (UNIQUE)
   - passwordHash, firstName, lastName, avatarUrl, phone, position
   - departmentId (FK), role (default 'user')
   - isActive, isEmailVerified, twoFactorEnabled, twoFactorSecret
   - lastLoginAt, lastLoginIp, failedLoginAttempts, lockedUntil
   - createdAt, updatedAt

6. userSessions
   - id (UUID PK), userId (FK), token (UNIQUE), refreshToken
   - userAgent, ipAddress, lastActivityAt, expiresAt, createdAt

7. emailVerificationTokens
   - id (UUID PK), userId (FK), token (UNIQUE), expiresAt, createdAt

8. passwordResetTokens
   - id (UUID PK), userId (FK), token (UNIQUE), expiresAt, usedAt

--- FOLDERS & HIERARCHY ---

9. folders
   - id (UUID PK), organizationId (FK), name
   - parentId (self-reference), path (materialized path)
   - depth, sortOrder, color, icon, description
   - createdBy, isActive, createdAt, updatedAt

10. folderTags
    - id (UUID PK), folderId (FK), tag, color, createdAt

--- PERMISSIONS ---

11. folderPermissions
    - id (UUID PK), folderId (FK), teamId (FK), userId (FK)
    - permissionType, inheritToSubfolders (default true)
    - grantedBy, expiresAt, createdAt

12. customRoles
    - id (UUID PK), organizationId (FK), name, description
    - color (default 'indigo'), isActive, createdBy, createdAt

13. rolePermissions
    - id (UUID PK), roleId (FK), resourceType ('folder'|'document')
    - resourceId, permissionLevel ('none'|'viewer'|'editor'|'admin')

14. userRoleAssignments
    - id (UUID PK), userId (FK), roleId (FK)
    - assignedBy, assignedAt

--- DOCUMENTS & VERSIONING ---

15. documents
    - id (UUID PK), organizationId (FK), folderId (FK)
    - filename, originalFilename, mimeType
    - fileSize (BIGINT), currentVersionId, versionCount (default 1)
    - status (default 'active'), ownerId (FK)
    - isPublic, contentHash, ssdeepHash, simHash, expiresAt
    - aiAccessGranted, aiAccessGrantedAt, aiAccessGrantedBy
    - blockchainAnchored (BOOLEAN, default false), blockchainAnchorId (FK)
    - autoAnchorOnEdit (default false)
    - editLockedBy (FK→users, ON DELETE SET NULL), editLockAcquiredAt, editLockExpiresAt
      (OnlyOffice collaboration edit lock, §26; index doc_lock_expires_idx)
    - trackingEnabled (BOOLEAN, default false) — OSINT tracking toggle (see §49)
    - trackingCode (TEXT, null until enabled) — stable per-doc discovery code (docCode) (§49)
    - qrOnDownload (BOOLEAN, default false) — embed verification QR onto downloads (§49)
    - deletedAt, deletedBy, createdAt, updatedAt

16. documentVersions
    - id (UUID PK), documentId (FK), versionNumber
    - s3Key, s3Bucket, fileSize
    - encryptionKeyId, encryptionIv, encryptionSalt
    - sha256Hash, bodyHash
    - archiveStatus (default 'active'), archivedAt, archiveJobId (AWS Glacier job ref)
    - createdBy (FK), changeNote, createdAt

17. documentPermissions
    - id (UUID PK), documentId (FK), teamId (FK), userId (FK)
    - permissionType, grantedBy, expiresAt, createdAt

--- DOCUMENT SECURITY ---

18. documentWatermarks
    - id (UUID PK), documentId (FK), versionId (FK)
    - watermarkType ('lsb_steganography')
    - payloadHash, embeddingPositions (JSONB), redundancyLevel
    - imageCount, imageDetails (JSONB), isActive, createdAt

19. documentHoneytokens
    - id (UUID PK), documentId (FK), versionId (FK)
    - zwcToken, zwcPositions (JSONB)
    - homoglyphToken, homoglyphPositions (JSONB)
    - whitespaceToken, whitespacePositions (JSONB)
    - combinedPayloadHash, isActive, createdAt

20. documentQrCodes
    - id (UUID PK), documentId (FK), versionId (FK)
    - payloadHash, signatureHash (HMAC-SHA256)
    - verificationUrl, shortCode (UNIQUE)
    - qrImageS3Key, hasBlockchainProof
    - scanCount, lastScannedAt, isActive, createdAt

20b. downloadWatermarks  *** NEW — Per-Download Watermarking ***
    - id (UUID PK), documentId (FK→documents), versionId (FK→documentVersions)
    - downloadedBy (FK→users, ON DELETE SET NULL)
    - watermarkId (TEXT, UNIQUE) — UUID per download event
    - watermarkToken (TEXT) — SHA-256 of positions+payload
    - watermarkPositions (JSONB) — array of insertion indices
    - payloadHash (TEXT) — SHA-256 of full payload
    - payload (JSONB) — {w: watermarkId, d: docId, u: userId, t: timestamp}
    - documentFormat (TEXT) — 'text_direct'|'docx_xml'|'pdf_annotation'|etc.
                              (for visible-code rows: stamp method, e.g. 'text_visible'|'pdf_visible')
    - watermarkMethod (TEXT, default 'unicode_invisible')
    - visibleCode (TEXT, null if none) — full visible OSINT canary code stamped on this download (§49)
    - ipAddress (TEXT), userAgent (TEXT)
    - isActive (BOOLEAN, default true), createdAt (TIMESTAMP)
    Indexes: documentId, downloadedBy, watermarkId (unique), payloadHash

21. blockchainAnchors
    - id (UUID PK), documentId (FK), versionId (FK)
    - blockchainNetwork ('polygon'), transactionHash (UNIQUE)
    - blockNumber, blockTimestamp, anchoredHash
    - pseudonymizedOrgId, merkleRoot, merkleProof (JSONB)
    - status ('pending'|'confirmed'|'failed')
    - confirmations, gasUsed, gasCost, createdAt, confirmedAt

--- DOCUMENT SHARING ---

22. documentShares
    - id (UUID PK), documentId (FK)
    - shareType, shareToken (UNIQUE), shareUrl
    - allowedEmails (JSONB), requireAuth (default true)
    - maxViews, viewCount, expiresAt
    - createdBy (FK), isActive, createdAt

23. documentShareAccess
    - id (UUID PK), shareId (FK), accessedBy (FK)
    - ipAddress, userAgent, accessedAt

--- VERIFICATION ---

24. verificationRequests
    - id (UUID PK), inputMethod ('qr_scan'|'upload_softcopy'|
      'upload_hardcopy'|'manual_hash')
    - inputHash, inputQrCode, uploadedFileS3Key
    - status, matchedDocumentId (FK), matchedVersionId (FK)
    - isHardcopyScan, ssdeepSimilarity (%), simHashDistance
    - chunkAnalysis (JSONB), differingChunks (JSONB)
    - blockchainVerified, blockchainAnchorId (FK)
    - requestedBy (FK), ipAddress, resultMessage, resultDetails (JSONB)
    - completedAt, createdAt
    (NOTE: inputMethod 'upload_softcopy' is the current file-verify value; §48)

--- TRASH & COMPLIANCE ---

25. trashItems
    - id (UUID PK), organizationId (FK)
    - itemType ('document'|'folder'), itemId
    - originalFolderId, originalPath, itemMetadata (JSONB)
    - autoDeleteAt (30 days), deletedBy (FK), deletedAt

26. cryptoShredding
    - id (UUID PK), organizationId, targetType, targetId
    - kmsKeyIds (JSONB), status ('pending'|'completed'|'failed')
    - reason, requestedBy (FK)
    - affectedDocuments, affectedVersions, completedAt, createdAt

--- FORMS & TEMPLATES ---

27. templates
    - id (UUID PK), organizationId (FK), name, description
    - category, s3Key, mimeType, thumbnailUrl
    - isSystemTemplate, isPublic, usageCount
    - createdBy (FK), isActive, createdAt, updatedAt

28. forms
    - id (UUID PK), organizationId (FK), title, description
    - icon, category, schema (JSONB), uiSchema (JSONB)
    - isPublic, requireAuth, allowAnonymous
    - maxSubmissions, submissionCount, usageCount
    - linkedTemplateId (FK), createdBy (FK), isActive, expiresAt

29. formSubmissions
    - id (UUID PK), formId (FK)

================================================================================
5. DOCUMENT UPLOAD PIPELINE (15 STEPS)
================================================================================

File: backend/src/services/upload-pipeline.service.js

User Upload via HTTPS (React -> Express)
       |
       v
[1] VALIDATE
    - MIME type whitelist check
    - Magic bytes verification (file-type library)
    - File size <= 50MB
       |
       v
[2] TEMP STORE
    - Buffer saved to /storage/temp/{sessionId}{ext}
    - Record created in temporaryUploads table (status: scanning)
       |
       v
[3] CLAMAV SCAN
    - TCP INSTREAM protocol to ClamAV container :3310
    - 2KB chunked streaming
    - CLEAN -> continue
    - INFECTED -> secureWipe(tempPath) -> reject with threat name
       |
       v
[4] EXTRACT TEXT  (extractText, category-routed — see §32)
    - PDF (text)      : pdfjs per-page
    - PDF (scanned)   : pdfjs render → OCR (tesseract.js)
    - Images          : OCR (PNG/JPEG/WebP/TIFF/BMP)
    - DOCX/DOC        : mammoth
    - ODT/RTF         : jszip XML / RTF strip
    - XLSX/XLS/ODS    : SheetJS (xlsx) → CSV text
    - PPTX/ODP        : jszip slide XML
    - TXT/CSV/MD      : raw UTF-8
    - Unreadable      : skip (raw-byte hash fallback)
    Note: dangerous types (exe/bat/sh/dll/…) already rejected at validation by
    blocklist + magic-byte check (single allow/block list in upload.config.js).
       |
       v
[5] NORMALIZE
    - 7-step normalization:
      1. Lowercase
      2. Collapse whitespace
      3. Strip punctuation
      4. Strip special characters
      5. Unicode NFKD normalization
      6. Strip diacritics
      7. Final trim
       |
       v
[6] DOCUMENT DNA
    - SHA-256 (exact match)
    - SimHash 64-bit (similarity detection — primary fuzzy signal)
    - SSDEEP (pure-JS spamsum, homegrown; stored in ssdeepHash; tie-breaker only — see §8.5)
    - Hash computed from normalizedText (or raw buffer if no text)
       |
       v
[7] DUPLICATE CHECK
    - Query DB for exact SHA-256 match
    - Check SimHash Hamming distance <= 10 (~84% similar)
    - Warning if duplicate found (upload continues)
       |
       v
[8] HONEYTOKEN INJECT (text files only)
    - Method 1: Zero-Width Characters (U+200B/C/D, U+FEFF) at 8 positions
    - Method 2: Homoglyph substitution (Latin->Cyrillic) at 15 positions
    - Method 3: Whitespace encoding (trailing space=0, tab=1) at 20 lines
    - Combined payload hash stored for validation
       |
       v
[9] QR CODE
    - HMAC-SHA256 signed payload
    - QR image generated -> stored in R2 qr bucket
    - 8-char short code for the owner-only verification endpoint
       |
       v
[10] ENCRYPT
     - Generate per-document key via Vault Transit (or local fallback)
     - AES-256-GCM encryption
     - Produces: encryptedBuffer + authTag
     - Plaintext key exists ONLY in memory, encrypted key stored in DB
       |
       v
[11] STORE TO MINIO
     - PutObject to bucket "docloq-documents"
     - Key: {documentId}/{timestamp}{ext}.enc
     - Metadata sidecar: .meta.json (authTag, originalName, mimeType)
       |
       v
[12] DATABASE INSERT
     - Atomic transaction:
       documents + documentVersions + documentHoneytokens +
       documentQrCodes + auditLogs + update temporaryUploads
       |
       v
[13] SECURE WIPE
     - Overwrite temp file with zeros (64KB chunks) -> unlink
     - 3-pass secure deletion
       |
       v
[14] VECTOR INDEX (DEFERRED)
     - Log: "Vector indexing deferred (requires user AI access grant)"
     - Only indexed when user explicitly grants AI access
       |
       v
[15] RETURN RESPONSE
     - { document, version, qrCode, dna, encryption, storage, warnings }

Supported MIME Types:
  - application/pdf
  - application/msword, application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.*
  - application/vnd.ms-excel, application/vnd.ms-powerpoint
  - text/plain, text/csv, text/rtf
  - image/png, image/jpeg
  - application/vnd.oasis.opendocument.*

================================================================================
6. DOCUMENT DOWNLOAD FLOW (WITH PER-DOWNLOAD WATERMARKING)
================================================================================

GET /api/documents/:id/download
       |
       v
Auth check (JWT) + Permission check (owner / team / folder permission)
       |
       v
Query documentVersions -> s3Key, encryptionKeyId, encryptionIv
       |
       v
R2/S3 GetObject -> encrypted buffer
       |
       v
Read .meta.json sidecar -> authTag
       |
       v
Vault Transit decryptDataKey(encryptedKey) -> plaintextKey
  (or local fallback: decryptDocumentKey)
       |
       v
decryptFile(encryptedBuffer, plaintextKey, iv, authTag) -> plaintext buffer
       |
       v
*** NEW: PER-DOWNLOAD INVISIBLE WATERMARK INJECTION ***
       |
       +---> Generate watermarkId (UUID)
       +---> Build payload: {w: watermarkId, d: docId, u: downloaderId, t: ISO timestamp}
       +---> watermarkBuffer(decryptedBuffer, mimeType, watermarkFn)
       |        |
       |        +---> TXT: direct UTF-8 string injection
       |        +---> DOCX: JSZip → word/document.xml → inject into <w:t> elements
       |        +---> PDF: pdf-lib → drawText(invisible, opacity:0) on page 1
       |        +---> Other: skip watermarking, serve original
       |
       +---> Invisible chars: U+2060, U+2061, U+2062, U+2063
       |     (non-overlapping with upload honeytoken chars U+200B-FEFF)
       |
       +---> Fire-and-forget: INSERT into downloadWatermarks table
       +---> Include watermarkId in audit log
       |
       v
Set Content-Disposition + Content-Type headers -> stream watermarked buffer

Also applies to: GET /api/documents/:id/download-as (converted downloads)
Does NOT apply to: GET /api/documents/:id/file (OnlyOffice serving)

================================================================================
7. AI DOCUMENT ANALYSIS FLOW
================================================================================

--- AI ACCESS GRANT SYSTEM ---

Security concept: Documents are BLOCKED from AI by default.
User must explicitly grant access before AI can process them.

Rationale:
  - Data sovereignty: user controls if content is processed by AI
  - GDPR Art 6 compliance: consent before processing
  - Defense-in-depth: encrypted docs in R2 not auto-exposed to AI

Flow:

  User Upload Document
         |
         v
  [15-step pipeline: scan, honeytoken, encrypt, store R2/S3, DB, etc. — see §5]
         |
         v
  Document appears on AI Analysis page -> STATUS: BLOCKED (locked)
         |
         v
  User clicks document -> Modal "Grant AI Access?"
    +-- "Grant" -> decrypt -> extract text -> embed -> upsert Qdrant
    |                         aiAccessGranted = true in DB
    |                         v
    |              Document STATUS: GRANTED (green)
    |              User can send prompts for analysis
    |
    +-- "Keep Blocked" -> no action, document stays locked

--- GRANT AI ACCESS FLOW ---

POST /api/ai-analysis/documents/:id/grant
  1. Verify user has permission for this document
  2. Get latest document version -> s3Key, encryptionKeyId, encryptionIv
  3. Download encrypted file from R2/S3
  4. Read .meta.json sidecar -> authTag
  5. Decrypt with Vault/local key
  6. Extract text (pdf-parse / raw text)
  7. PII REDACTION — the caller passes redactionMode, 'censored' (default) or
     'full'. 'censored' runs redactForOutbound() (pii-redaction.service.js) over
     the extracted text first; email / phone / NIK / NPWP counts are recorded.
     'full' sends the raw text. Nothing leaves the server un-redacted unless the
     user explicitly chose 'full'. Stored on the document as aiRedactionMode.
  8. Generate embedding via OpenAI (text-embedding-3-small) from the REDACTED text
  9. Upsert to Qdrant Cloud (with organizationId filter)
  10. Update DB: aiAccessGranted = true, aiRedactionMode
  11. Audit log: action = 'ai_grant_access' (records redactionMode + PII counts)

--- ANALYZE DOCUMENT FLOW ---

POST /api/ai-analysis/documents/:id/analyze
  1. Sanitize prompt input
  2. Detect prompt injection (15 regex patterns)
  3. Verify aiAccessGranted = true
  4. Check MongoDB cache (key = SHA-256(documentId + normalized_prompt))
     - Cache HIT -> return cached result + record history
  5. If cache MISS:
     a. Decrypt document (download -> decrypt -> extract text)
     a2. redactForOutbound() again on the extracted text — the analyze path
         redacts unconditionally, it does not re-read aiRedactionMode
         (ai-analysis.service.js:485)
     b. Truncate to 30,000 chars if needed (ai-analysis.service.js maxContext=30000)
     c. Search Qdrant for similar documents (top 3)
     d. Build system prompt with chart generation rules
     e. Call OpenAI with structured JSON output — model AI_ANALYSIS_MODEL,
        default gpt-5 (ai-analysis.service.js:28). NOT gpt-4.1-mini.
     f. Parse response (summary, keyMetrics, sentiment, charts, etc.)
     g. Cache result in MongoDB (24h TTL)
     h. Record in analysis history (permanent)
     i. Write audit log
  6. Return structured analysis result

--- AI RESPONSE FORMAT ---

{
  "summary":           "2-3 sentence document summary",
  "keyMetrics":        [{ "label", "value", "icon" }],
  "sentimentData":     { "positive", "neutral", "negative" },
  "keyTopics":         ["topic1", "topic2", ...],
  "insights":          [{ "type": "info|success|warning", "text" }],
  "wordFrequency":     [{ "word", "count" }],
  "readabilityScore":  0-100,
  "complianceStatus":  "Verified|Needs Review|Non-Compliant",
  "charts": [
    {
      "id":            "unique_chart_id",
      "type":          "bar|line|pie|doughnut|radar|area|horizontalBar",
      "title":         "Chart title",
      "description":   "What the chart shows",
      "data": {
        "labels":      ["Label1", "Label2"],
        "datasets": [{
          "label":           "Dataset label",
          "data":            [10, 20, 30],
          "backgroundColor": ["#6366f1", "#8b5cf6"],
          "borderColor":     "#6366f1"
        }]
      }
    }
  ]
}

--- MONGODB CACHE SCHEMA ---

Collection: analysis_results (TTL-based, 24h default)
  - cacheKey (SHA-256 hash, unique index)
  - documentId, organizationId, userId, prompt
  - result (Mixed), model, tokensUsed
  - expiresAt (TTL index), hitCount, lastHitAt

Collection: analysis_history (permanent)
  - documentId, organizationId, userId, prompt
  - resultSummary, model, tokensUsed, cached

--- REVOKE AI ACCESS ---

POST /api/ai-analysis/documents/:id/revoke
  1. Delete vector from Qdrant
  2. Invalidate all MongoDB cache entries for document
  3. Set aiAccessGranted = false in DB
  4. Audit log: action = 'ai_revoke_access'

================================================================================
8. SECURITY FEATURES
================================================================================

8.1 ENCRYPTION
    - Algorithm: AES-256-GCM (authenticated encryption)
    - Pattern: Envelope encryption
      * Master key (Vault Transit or local) encrypts per-document keys
      * Per-document key encrypts file content
      * IV (Initialization Vector) unique per encryption
      * AuthTag stored in .meta.json sidecar
    - Key material never leaves Vault in production

8.2 MALWARE SCANNING
    - ClamAV integration via TCP INSTREAM protocol
    - Scans uploaded files BEFORE encryption
    - 2KB chunked streaming for memory efficiency
    - Infected files: secure-wiped immediately
    - Dev mode: can be disabled (SCANNER_ENABLED=false)

8.3 HONEYTOKENS (Document Leak Detection)
    - Three injection methods (invisible to reader):
      1. Zero-Width Characters: Unicode U+200B/C/D/FEFF at 8 positions
      2. Homoglyph Substitution: Latin->Cyrillic lookalikes at 15 positions
      3. Whitespace Encoding: trailing space/tab on 20 lines
    - Payload: documentId, userId, organizationId, timestamp
    - Combined payload hash for validation
    - Can trace leaked documents back to source user

8.4 QR CODE VERIFICATION
    - HMAC-SHA256 signed payload
    - Contains: documentId, SHA-256 hash, organization, timestamp
    - 8-character random short code for the owner-only verification URL
    - Timing-safe signature comparison
    - QR images stored in dedicated R2 bucket (docloq-qrcodes)
    - Decode endpoint (POST /api/documents/verify-qr-image) parses uploaded
      PNG/JPG via jsqr+sharp (see §30 for wiring detail, added 2026-05-24)
    - Every verification attempt (success/fail) logged to verificationRequests
      table with inputMethod, status, simHashDistance, blockchainAnchorId

8.5 DOCUMENT DNA (Hashing)
    - SHA-256: exact match detection
    - SimHash (64-bit): similarity detection. verification.service.js constants
      SIMHASH_VERIFIED_MAX_DIST=6, SIMHASH_PARTIAL_MAX_DIST=14 (findBySimHash status).
      NOTE: the separate dedup path hash.service.js checkSimilar uses dist≤10.
      NOTE (§48): the current verify-file VERDICT is decided by similarity %
      (decideFileVerdict: ≥95 verified_fuzzy, ≥78 partial), not the raw distance status.
    - SSDEEP: IMPLEMENTED, but as a self-contained pure-JS spamsum (hash.service.js
      generateSSDeep → "blocksize:h1:h2"). No native libfuzzy/ssdeep-nodejs dependency.
      CORRECTION (2026-06-30 audit): earlier docs said "placeholder / column always null"
      — that is STALE. ssdeepHash IS populated for text documents and ssdeepCompare()
      runs. HONEST CAVEATS:
        * NOT interoperable with standard ssdeep tooling (homegrown hash format).
        * Comparison uses normalized Levenshtein edit distance, NOT ssdeep's weighted
          scoring → similarity numbers are approximate, not real-ssdeep scores.
        * SECONDARY signal only — it does NOT decide the verdict. verify-file (§48) ranks
          by SimHash distance and decides via decideFileVerdict({exactMatch, similarity})
          where similarity = SimHash; ssdeep is only a tie-breaker / displayed value.
    - Applied to normalized text (7-step normalization)

8.6 SECURE DELETION
    - Zero-overwrite in 64KB chunks before unlinking
    - Applied to all temporary files after processing
    - Recursive directory wiping support

8.7 CRYPTO SHREDDING (GDPR Art 17)
    CORRECTION (2026-06-30 audit): archive.service.js cryptoShred() does NOT delete a
    key from Vault Transit. It NULLIFIES the per-version wrapped DEK reference
    (documentVersions.encryptionKeyId + encryptionIv) and sets archiveStatus='deleted'.
    Per the code comment, this build does not use per-document Vault keys, so nulling the
    wrapped DEK is the actual shred primitive: the AES-256-GCM ciphertext becomes
    unrecoverable even if the Vault master KEK still lives. vault.service.js cryptoShredKey()
    EXISTS but is never called (dead code). Honest mechanism:
    - Nullify wrapped DEK reference in DB (encryptionKeyId, encryptionIv) — the real shred
    - Delete vector embedding from Qdrant
    - Delete ciphertext from storage (S3)
    - Mark document/version as deleted
    - Full audit trail of shredding operation (cryptoShredding table)
    - NOTE: no Vault Transit key is destroyed in this build (none is per-document).

8.8 AUTHENTICATION & AUTHORIZATION
    - JWT-based session management (3-hour idle timeout)
    - Two-Factor Authentication (TOTP via authenticator app)
    - Email OTP as alternative 2FA method
    - Cloudflare Turnstile CAPTCHA (hCaptcha deprecated 2026-05-24, §29)
    - Role-based access control: 3 tenant roles — owner / admin / user (§42; was 6 pre-refactor)
    - Custom role system with resource-level permissions (customRoles/rolePermissions)
    - Folder permission inheritance to subfolders
    - Admin separate auth system with OTP

8.9 PROMPT INJECTION DETECTION
    - 15 regex patterns for common injection attempts
    - Input sanitization for all AI prompts
    - System prompt protection (never reveal internal instructions)
        
8.10 BLOCKCHAIN ANCHORING (Polygon)
     - Network: Polygon (mainnet chainId 137, Amoy testnet chainId 80002)
     - Smart Contract: DocLoqAnchor.sol (Solidity 0.8.20+)
     - Gas-optimized: all data as bytes32/uint (no string storage)
     - NO PII stored on-chain — only SHA-256 content hashes
     - Organization IDs pseudonymized via keccak256()
     - Packed structs (uint32+uint64+bool fit in 1 storage slot)
     - Batch anchoring via Merkle root (1 transaction for N documents)
     - Events used for historical data (cheaper than storage)
     - Access control: owner + authorized anchors mapping
     - Only admin/owner can manually anchor documents (user consent model)
     - Auto-anchor-on-edit toggle per document
     - Only admin/owner can verify document authenticity from blockchain
     - Backend: ethers.js v6 (JsonRpcProvider + Wallet + Contract)
     - Graceful degradation: BLOCKCHAIN_ENABLED=false until wallet configured

8.11 PER-DOWNLOAD INVISIBLE WATERMARKING (Leak Identification)
     - Purpose: Identify WHICH USER leaked a document (not just the uploader)
     - Separate from honeytokens (8.3): honeytokens identify uploader,
       download watermarks identify the specific downloader
     - Every download event creates a unique watermarked copy
     - Encoding: 4 Unicode invisible characters (U+2060-U+2063)
       * U+2060 Word Joiner
       * U+2061 Function Application
       * U+2062 Invisible Times
       * U+2063 Invisible Separator
     - Non-overlapping with honeytoken chars (U+200B/C/D/FEFF)
     - 2-bit-per-char encoding (same scheme as ZWC honeytokens)
     - Payload: {watermarkId, documentId, downloaderId, timestamp}
     - Randomized injection positions via crypto.randomInt()
       (prevents diff-comparison watermark stripping)
     - 12 injection points distributed across document text
     - Format support: TXT (direct), DOCX (XML modification), PDF (invisible text)
     - Scanner: upload suspected leaked file → extract → decode → DB lookup → identify
     - Dual-layer detection: scans for both download watermarks AND upload honeytokens
     - Records stored in downloadWatermarks table with IP, user agent, payload hash

================================================================================
9. BACKEND SERVICES (46 SERVICE FILES — 44 *.service.js + 2 *.util.js)
================================================================================

File: backend/src/services/

NOTE: The numbered list below is partial / historical (curated, ~23 entries). As of
the 2026-06-30 audit there are 46 files on disk. Services NOT in the numbered list:
  - verification.service.js   (§30/§48 — exact SHA + SimHash fuzzy, QR decode, verdicts)
  - turnstile.service.js      (§29 — Cloudflare Turnstile siteverify)
  - ai-project.service.js / ai-project-chat.service.js / ai-project-note.service.js (§28,§31)
  - ai-cache.service.js, url-ingest.service.js
  - document-lock.service.js / document-presence.service.js / document-comment.service.js (§26)
  - share.service.js, share-preview.service.js  (§36/§40 — public share + render)
  - notification.service.js, archive.service.js, leak-scanner.service.js
  - auth-2fa-ticket.service.js  (§34 — 2FA login ticket)
  - merkle.util.js, totp.util.js, ssrf-guard.js
  - osint-discovery.service.js / google-search.service.js / tracking-code.service.js (§49)
  - auth-session.service.js   (UNDOCUMENTED until now — shared session/JWT issuer for
                               password login + Google OAuth, writes userSessions rows)
  - google-oauth.service.js   (UNDOCUMENTED until now — Google OAuth2 URL/code-exchange
                               + id_token verification; powers /auth/google/success)
  - watermark-extract.service.js (UNDOCUMENTED until now — multi-source forensic text
                               extraction for the scanner: PDF keyword metadata, DOCX
                               custom XML, TXT, preserving invisible Unicode chars)
  - recaptcha.service.js      (LEGACY — present on disk but NOT imported anywhere; the
                               active CAPTCHA is Turnstile. Effectively dead code.)
  - github-leak-scanner.service.js  (REMOVED 2026-06-28, replaced by osint-discovery, §49)


1.  upload-pipeline.service.js

    Vector database for AI semantic search (OpenAI embeddings)

    AI document analysis with GPT-4.1-mini, charts, MongoDB cache

13. ai-cache.service.js

    MongoDB caching for AI analysis results (24h TTL)

    DoKi chatbot: intent classification, LLM integration, security

    - findRelevantFeature(message)
    - findFAQMatch(message)
    - formatFeatureResponse(feature)
    - formatRolesResponse()
    Static knowledge base for DoKi chatbot

    - verifyEmailConnection()
    Email delivery service (Nodemailer/SMTP)

    - downloadFromUrl(url)
    Document conversion via OnlyOffice API

18. archive.service.js

    - restoreVersion(versionId, userId)

    - archiveOldVersions(olderThanDays, systemUserId)
    Archive, restore, and crypto shredding (GDPR)

19. docuseal.service.js
    - createSubmissionFromPDF(pdfBuffer, documentName, signer, options)
    - createSubmissionFromTemplate(templateId, signer, options)
    - getSubmission(submissionId)
    - getSubmissionDocuments(submissionId)
    - getSubmitter(submitterId)
    - listSubmitters(submissionId)
    - createTemplateFromPDF(pdfBuffer, name, options)
    - listTemplates(options)
    - getTemplate(templateId)
    - downloadSignedDocument(url, savePath)
    DocuSeal e-signature API wrapper

20. download-watermark.service.js  *** NEW ***
    - injectDownloadWatermark(textContent, payload)
      Injects invisible U+2060-U+2063 chars into text at random positions
      Returns: { modifiedText, watermarkToken, positions }
    - extractDownloadWatermark(textContent)
      Scans text for invisible sequences, decodes payload
      Returns: { payload, confidence, positions }
    - hashPayload(payload)
      SHA-256 hash for DB storage
    Per-download invisible watermark injection & extraction

21. document-rewriter.service.js  *** NEW ***
    - watermarkBuffer(buffer, mimeType, watermarkFn)
      Format-aware watermark injection into document buffers
      Strategies:
        TXT  → direct UTF-8 string manipulation
        DOCX → JSZip: unzip → modify word/document.xml <w:t> nodes → re-zip
        PDF  → pdf-lib: invisible text annotation (opacity:0) + metadata keyword
        XLSX/PPTX → metadata-only (custom XML part in ZIP archive)
      Returns: { buffer, method, success, watermarkToken, positions }

22. ocr.service.js
    - parsePdf(buffer)
      pdfjs per-page text extraction → { text, numpages, pageTexts }
    - getPageCount(buffer, mimeType)
    - detectContentType(buffer, mimeType)
      Per-page text-vs-image detection → 'full-text' | 'mixed' | 'image-only'
    - extractPageAsImage(pdfBuffer, pageIndex)
      Render PDF page → PNG via pdfjs + @napi-rs/canvas (OCR_RENDER_DPI, default 300)
    - detectAndCorrectRotation(buffer)   EXIF auto-rotate
    - correctOrientationOSD(buffer)
      Tesseract OSD (Legacy engine + osd.traineddata) → upright 0/90/180/270
    - enhanceImage(buffer, { aggressive }) grayscale→normalize→CLAHE→sharpen→upscale;
      aggressive adds median denoise + binarize
    - performOCR(buffer, lang, { dpi })  Tesseract recognize, PSM 6 + user_defined_dpi
    - processImageForAI(buffer, lang, { dpi })
      Full pipeline: EXIF → OSD → enhance → OCR → (low-conf) aggressive retry → cleanOcrText
    - extractTextFromPages(pdfBuffer, pageNumbers, pageDetails)
      Per-page: text pages direct, image pages render→OCR
    - cleanOcrText(text)  join hyphenation, drop noise lines, normalize whitespace
    OCR engine: tesseract.js WASM (self-hosted), eng+ind+osd in vendor/tessdata/.
    Two reusable workers (LSTM recognize + Legacy OSD), 5-min idle auto-terminate.
    Env: OCR_LANG, OCR_TESSDATA_PATH, OCR_MIN_CONFIDENCE, OCR_ENABLE_OSD, OCR_RENDER_DPI.
    See §32 for the 2026-05-29 accuracy upgrade.

23. blockchain.service.js  *** NEW ***
    - initBlockchain()
      Connect to Polygon RPC, load wallet from env, instantiate contract
    - anchorDocument(documentId, versionId, contentHash, organizationId, versionNumber)
      Anchor single document hash to Polygon, insert into blockchainAnchors table
    - updateAnchor(documentId, newContentHash, newVersionNumber)
      Re-anchor document after edit (for auto-anchor-on-edit feature)
    - verifyDocument(documentId, contentHash)
      View call to smart contract (no gas), verify hash matches on-chain record
    - anchorBatch(docs)
      Merkle root batch anchoring: 1 transaction for N documents
      Generates individual merkle proofs per document
    - getBlockchainStats()
      Wallet balance, total anchored, pending txs, gas totals from DB
    - getTransactionHistory(limit, offset)
      Paginated transaction history from blockchainAnchors table
    Helpers: toAnchorId(), pseudonymizeOrgId(), hashToBytes32()
    Merkle tree: computeMerkleRoot(), computeMerkleProof()
    Background tx confirmation tracking: confirmTransaction()

Base URL: /api
Server: Express 5 on port 3000

  COUNT NOTE (audited 2026-06-30): there are 211 router handlers across 24 route
  files (mounted under /api by routes/index.js). The detailed listing below covers
  the core surface; route groups added later are summarised in §10.X "ADDITIONAL
  ROUTE GROUPS" near the end of this section and detailed in their feature sections.

--- AUTHENTICATION (/api/auth) ---

POST   /api/auth/login              Login with email/password
POST   /api/auth/complete-login     Complete 2FA login flow
POST   /api/auth/register           Register new user
                                    Body: { email, password, firstName, lastName, companyName? }
                                    Note: Use 'companyName' (not 'orgName') to create an org.
                                          First user of an org gets 'admin' role automatically.

POST   /api/auth/logout             [Auth] Logout user

--- DOCUMENTS (/api/documents) ---
  Note: Document routes use optionalAuth. Unauthenticated requests can list/read
        documents but are filtered to public documents only. Authenticated users
        see documents scoped to their organization + permission level.
        VERIFICATION IS OWNER-ONLY (corrected §66.C). Every verification path —
        short code, QR image, file upload, preview, blockchain-integrity —
        requires authenticate + authorize(['owner']) + requireFeature('verification').
        A QR scan by a non-owner, or a direct API call, is rejected.

GET    /api/documents               List all documents
GET    /api/documents/verify        Verify by QR short code (auto-resolves chain) [OWNER]
POST   /api/documents/verify-qr-image     Verify by uploaded QR PNG/JPG (auto-chain) [OWNER]
POST   /api/documents/verify-file         Verify by uploaded digital file: exact SHA-256
                                          + SimHash fuzzy, org-scoped              [OWNER]
GET    /api/documents/:id/blockchain-integrity  On-demand chain integrity check    [OWNER]
GET    /api/documents/:id/verify-preview  Read-only rendered preview (View document) [opt-auth]
PATCH  /api/documents/:id/tracking         Toggle OSINT tracking (§49)  [auth, feat:tracking]
PATCH  /api/documents/:id/qr-on-download    Toggle QR-on-download (§49)  [auth, feat:qr]
   NOTE (2026-06-28, §48): POST /verify-by-hash and POST /verify-hardcopy were REMOVED
   and replaced by the single POST /verify-file (login-required, org-scoped).
GET    /api/documents/:id           Get document metadata (+ qrCode + blockchainAnchor)
GET    /api/documents/:id/qr-image  Serve QR PNG image                   [opt-auth]
GET    /api/documents/:id/file      Serve document file (OnlyOffice)
POST   /api/documents/upload        Upload document (full pipeline)
POST   /api/documents/upload-simple Simple upload (legacy)
GET    /api/documents/:id/versions  Get version history

11. FRONTEND FEATURES & PAGES
================================================================================

Framework: React 19 + Vite + TailwindCSS + Framer Motion
State: Zustand
Routing: React Router v7

--- PUBLIC PAGES ---

/                  Landing Page
                   - Hero section with animated text
                   - Feature showcase cards
                   - Call-to-action buttons
                   - AOS scroll animations

/contact           Contact Page
                   - Contact form (name, email, company, phone, subject)
                   - Company contact info
                   - FAQ section

/login             User Login
                   - Email/password input
                   - hCaptcha integration
                   - Remember me checkbox
                   - Dev bypass (Ctrl+Shift+D)

/verify-otp        OTP Verification
                   - 6-digit code input with auto-focus
                   - Authenticator app or Email OTP
                   - Auto-submit when all digits filled
                   - Resend with 60s countdown

--- DASHBOARD PAGES (Protected) ---

/dashboard         Main Dashboard
                   - Stats cards (Documents, Tasks, Folders, Storage,
                     Upload, Verification, AI Analysis, OSINT)
                   - Recent activity feed with timestamps
                   - Search/filter functionality

/documents         Document Management
                   - File type filtering (PDF, Word, Excel, PPT, Image)
                   - List/grid view toggle
                   - Upload documents (drag & drop)
                   - OnlyOffice integration for preview/edit
                   - Breadcrumb navigation
                   - Document actions (view, edit, delete, share)

/folders           Folder Hierarchy
                   - Tree structure visualization
                   - Expandable/collapsible nodes
                   - Folder statistics (depth, count, empty)
                   - Drag-and-drop support
                   - Create, rename, delete folders

/tasks             Task Management

================================================================================
13. AUTHENTICATION & AUTHORIZATION
================================================================================

--- AUTHENTICATION FLOW ---

1. User submits email + password (+ Cloudflare Turnstile if enabled; hCaptcha deprecated §29)
2. Server verifies credentials
3. If 2FA enabled:
   a. Return a 2FA ticket (auth-2fa-ticket.service.js) — see §34
   b. User enters TOTP code (authenticator) or email OTP
   c. Server verifies code
4. JWT token issued — stored in localStorage on the frontend (auth.store.js).
   CORRECTION (2026-06-30 audit): the backend does NOT set an auth cookie
   (no res.cookie in auth.controller.js); the old "cookie + localStorage" claim is wrong.
5. Session created in userSessions table
6. 3-hour idle timeout enforced

--- MIDDLEWARE ---

authenticate         Verify JWT, enforce idle timeout, attach req.user
authorize(roles)     Role-based access — valid tenant roles are ['owner','admin','user']
                     (e.g. authorize(['owner']) gates blockchain/osint/watermark-scanner)
optionalAuth         Allow unauthenticated access, attach user if valid
authenticateAdmin    Verify admin JWT + admin session (platform admin_users system)
requireSuperAdmin    Enforce platform super-admin (separate admin_users auth, NOT a tenant role)

--- PERMISSION SYSTEM ---

getUserPermissionLevel(userId, resourceType, resourceId, organizationId)
  -> Returns: 'none' | 'viewer' | 'editor' | 'admin'

Permission cascade:
  - Owner/admin: full access always
  - Custom roles: resource-level permissions
  - Folder permissions: inherit to subfolders + contained documents
  - Document permissions: direct assignment

requireDocumentPermission(minLevel)  Enforce on document routes
requireFolderPermission(minLevel)    Enforce on folder routes

--- ROLES ---

Built-in tenant roles: owner, admin, user  (user_role enum, schema.js — see §42 refactor).
   CORRECTION (2026-06-30 audit): the old list "super_admin, admin, manager, user, auditor,
   viewer" was obsolete/fabricated — those roles do NOT exist in the enum. Exactly one OWNER
   per tenant; platform super-admin is a separate admin_users system (§44), not a tenant role.
Custom roles: organization-level, with per-resource permissions (customRoles/rolePermissions/
   userRoleAssignments tables).

================================================================================
15. INVISIBLE DOWNLOAD WATERMARKING (LEAK IDENTIFICATION)
================================================================================

--- PROBLEM ---

DocLoq already has upload-time honeytokens (Section 8.3) that encode the
UPLOADER's identity into every document. However, when 5 users download the
same document, they all receive identical copies. If a document leaks, the
honeytoken can only identify who uploaded it — not who leaked it.

--- SOLUTION ---

Per-download invisible watermarking injects a UNIQUE watermark into every
downloaded copy, encoding the DOWNLOADER's identity. Each download event
produces a distinctly watermarked file. A scanner tool allows admins to
upload a suspected leaked document and identify the specific leaker.

--- ARCHITECTURE ---

    UPLOAD TIME (existing):              DOWNLOAD TIME (new):
    honeytoken.service.js                download-watermark.service.js
    Chars: U+200B, U+200C, U+200D, FEFF Chars: U+2060, U+2061, U+2062, U+2063
    Payload: {uploader}                  Payload: {downloader, watermarkId}
    Same for all copies                  Unique per download event
    Stored: documentHoneytokens table    Stored: downloadWatermarks table

--- ENCODING SCHEME ---

Characters (2-bit encoding, same approach as ZWC honeytokens):
  00 → U+2060 (Word Joiner)
  01 → U+2061 (Function Application)
  10 → U+2062 (Invisible Times)
  11 → U+2063 (Invisible Separator)

These characters are:
  - Invisible in all document viewers (Word, PDF readers, browsers)
  - Non-overlapping with existing honeytoken characters
  - Preserved through copy-paste operations
  - Part of the Unicode standard (General Punctuation block)

Payload format: JSON → 8 bits per character → 2-bit pairs → invisible chars
  { "w": "watermarkId-uuid", "d": "docId", "u": "userId", "t": "ISO-timestamp" }

--- INJECTION PROCESS ---

1. Generate watermarkId (UUID) for this download event
2. Build payload: {w, d, u, t}
3. Encode payload → bits → invisible character string
4. Split into 12 chunks (configurable positionCount)
5. Generate random insertion positions via crypto.randomInt()
   (truly random — makes diff-comparison stripping impractical)
6. Insert chunks at positions throughout document text
7. Rewrite document buffer with watermarked text:
   - TXT: direct string replacement, re-encode UTF-8
   - DOCX: unzip → modify word/document.xml → inject into first <w:t>
           element with xml:space="preserve" → re-zip
   - PDF: pdf-lib drawText(invisible, x:0, y:0, size:1, opacity:0)
          on first page + embed in PDF keywords metadata
   - XLSX/PPTX: inject via custom XML part in ZIP archive
8. Store watermark record in downloadWatermarks table (fire-and-forget)
9. Include watermarkId in audit log
10. Stream watermarked buffer to user

--- EXTRACTION / SCANNING PROCESS ---

1. Admin uploads suspected leaked document to scanner
2. Extract text from document (same extractText() as upload pipeline)
3. Scan for download watermarks: regex /[\u2060-\u2063]+/g
4. Scan for upload honeytokens: regex /[\u200B\u200C\u200D\uFEFF]+/g
5. Decode invisible chars → bits → JSON → payload
6. Validate payload structure (watermarkId, docId, userId, timestamp)
7. Calculate confidence score (0-1) based on payload completeness
8. DB lookup: watermarkId → downloadWatermarks → JOIN users
9. Return results:
   - Download watermark found: downloader name, email, timestamp, IP, confidence
   - Upload honeytoken found: uploader name, method, confidence
   - Nothing found: "document may have been sanitized or is not from this system"

--- FILES ---

Backend:
  backend/src/services/download-watermark.service.js   Inject/extract watermarks
  backend/src/services/document-rewriter.service.js    Format-specific buffer rewriting
  backend/src/controllers/watermark-scanner.controller.js  Scanner API endpoints
  backend/src/routes/watermark-scanner.routes.js       Route definitions
  backend/src/config/upload.config.js                  downloadWatermark config section
  backend/src/db/schema.js                             downloadWatermarks table

Frontend:
  frontend/src/services/watermark-scanner.service.js   API client
  frontend/src/features/osint-tracker/OSINTTracker.jsx Scan Watermark tab

--- API ENDPOINTS ---

POST   /api/watermark-scanner/scan                    [Admin+] Upload & scan file
GET    /api/watermark-scanner/history/:documentId     [Admin+] Download history
GET    /api/watermark-scanner/details/:watermarkId    [Admin+] Watermark record details

--- CONFIGURATION ---

Environment variables:
  DOWNLOAD_WATERMARK_ENABLED=true|false  (default: true)

Config (upload.config.js → downloadWatermark):
  enabled: true                          Toggle watermarking on/off
  chars: [U+2060, U+2061, U+2062, U+2063]  Invisible encoding characters
  positionCount: 12                      Number of injection points
  supportedCategories: [pdf, office, text]  File types to watermark

--- ANTI-STRIPPING MEASURES ---

1. Randomized positions: Each download uses crypto.randomInt() for positions,
   so comparing two copies doesn't reveal a consistent pattern to strip.
2. Dual layer: Upload honeytokens (3 methods) survive independently of
   download watermarks — stripping one layer still leaves the other.
3. Multiple injection points (12 by default): Partial text extraction or
   truncation still captures enough watermark fragments to decode.
4. Different encoding per format: PDF uses text annotation + metadata,
   DOCX uses XML text nodes — format conversion may preserve at least one.

--- PERFORMANCE ---

Estimated overhead per download:
  - Text extraction: ~100ms
  - Watermark injection: ~5ms
  - DOCX re-zip: ~100ms / PDF modification: ~150ms
  - DB insert (fire-and-forget): ~10ms
  Total: ~200-350ms (within 500ms budget)

================================================================================
16. BLOCKCHAIN DOCUMENT ANCHORING (POLYGON)
================================================================================

Purpose: Provide immutable proof of document authenticity and integrity
by anchoring document hashes to the Polygon blockchain.

--- SMART CONTRACT ---

File: backend/contracts/DocLoqAnchor.sol
ABI:  backend/contracts/DocLoqAnchor.json

Contract: DocLoqAnchor (Solidity 0.8.20+)

Storage (gas-optimized — all bytes32/uint, no strings):
  - mapping(bytes32 => DocumentAnchor) anchors
    * documentHash (bytes32)    SHA-256 hash of document content
    * pseudoOrgId  (bytes32)    keccak256 of organization UUID
    * versionNumber (uint32)    Document version at time of anchoring
    * timestamp     (uint64)    Block timestamp
    * exists        (bool)      Existence flag
  - mapping(bytes32 => MerkleAnchor) merkleAnchors
    * merkleRoot    (bytes32)   Root hash of batch
    * documentCount (uint32)    Number of documents in batch
    * timestamp     (uint64)    Block timestamp
    * exists        (bool)      Existence flag

Access Control:
  - owner: deployer address
  - mapping(address => bool) authorizedAnchors
  - onlyOwner: for authorize/revoke functions
  - onlyAuthorized: for anchoring operations

Functions:
  authorizeAnchor(address)    Owner adds backend wallet as authorized
  revokeAnchor(address)       Owner removes authorization
  anchorDocument(bytes32 anchorId, bytes32 docHash, bytes32 pseudoOrgId, uint32 version)
  updateAnchor(bytes32 anchorId, bytes32 newHash, uint32 newVersion)
  anchorBatch(bytes32 merkleRoot, uint32 docCount)
  verifyDocument(bytes32 anchorId, bytes32 docHash) view → (bool, uint64)
  verifyBatch(bytes32 merkleRoot) view → (bool, uint64, uint32)
  getAnchor(bytes32 anchorId) view → full anchor data
  transferOwnership(address newOwner)

Events:
  DocumentAnchored(bytes32 indexed anchorId, bytes32 docHash, uint64 timestamp)
  AnchorUpdated(bytes32 indexed anchorId, bytes32 newHash, uint32 newVersion)
  BatchAnchored(bytes32 indexed merkleRoot, uint32 docCount, uint64 timestamp)
  AnchorAuthorized(address indexed account)
  AnchorRevoked(address indexed account)
  OwnershipTransferred(address indexed prev, address indexed next)

Gas Optimizations:
  1. All data stored as bytes32/uint (no string storage)
  2. Packed struct: uint32 + uint64 + bool fit in single 32-byte slot
  3. Batch anchoring via Merkle root (1 tx for N documents)
  4. Events for historical data (cheaper than on-chain storage)
  5. View functions for verification (zero gas cost)

Data Protection (GDPR/Privacy):
  - NO PII stored on-chain (no names, emails, filenames)
  - Organization IDs pseudonymized: keccak256(orgUUID)
  - Only document content hashes stored (SHA-256, irreversible)
  - All identifiable data stays in PostgreSQL
  - Document-to-anchor mapping only in off-chain database

--- ANCHORING FLOW ---

  1. Admin clicks "Anchor to Polygon" on document detail modal
  2. Backend validates: document exists, has contentHash, user has admin role
  3. Generate anchorId = keccak256(documentId)
  4. Pseudonymize orgId = keccak256(organizationId)
  5. Call contract.anchorDocument(anchorId, hash, pseudoOrgId, version)
  6. Wait for transaction confirmation (configurable via BLOCKCHAIN_CONFIRMATIONS, default 2)
  7. Insert record into blockchainAnchors table
  8. Update documents.blockchainAnchored = true
  9. Return { txHash, blockNumber, gasUsed } to frontend

--- VERIFICATION FLOW ---

  1. Admin clicks "Verify Integrity" on anchored document
  2. Backend generates anchorId from documentId
  3. Call contract.verifyDocument(anchorId, currentContentHash) — view (no gas)
  4. Return { verified: bool, onChainHash, timestamp }
  5. Frontend shows green (match) or amber (mismatch) result

--- AUTO-ANCHOR ON EDIT ---

  1. Admin toggles "Auto-anchor on edit" switch in document modal
  2. Sets documents.autoAnchorOnEdit = true in database
  3. When document is saved via OnlyOffice callback:
     a. Upload pipeline processes new content
     b. If autoAnchorOnEdit=true AND BLOCKCHAIN_ENABLED=true:
        - Fire-and-forget call to blockchain.updateAnchor()
        - Re-anchors with new content hash and version number
  4. Keeps on-chain record synchronized with document edits

--- BATCH ANCHORING ---

  1. Admin selects multiple documents and uses "Batch Anchor"
  2. Backend builds Merkle tree from document content hashes
  3. Single transaction: contract.anchorBatch(merkleRoot, docCount)
  4. Individual Merkle proofs stored per document in blockchainAnchors
  5. Verification: reconstruct proof path to verify leaf against root
  6. Massive gas savings: 1 tx for 100+ documents

--- CONFIGURATION ---

Environment Variables (start on Amoy testnet first):
  BLOCKCHAIN_ENABLED=false                              (turn ON after deploy)
  POLYGON_RPC_URL=https://rpc-amoy.polygon.technology   (Amoy; mainnet: https://polygon-rpc.com)
  POLYGON_CHAIN_ID=80002                                (80002=Amoy testnet, 137=mainnet)
  POLYGON_PRIVATE_KEY=                                  (anchoring wallet key — NEVER commit)
  POLYGON_CONTRACT_ADDRESS=                             (filled in after deploy)
  BLOCKCHAIN_CONFIRMATIONS=2

Config: backend/src/config/upload.config.js → blockchain section
  enabled, rpcUrl, chainId, privateKey, contractAddress, confirmations (default 2)
  gasLimitSingle: 160000  (cold SSTORE ~104k + headroom; do NOT drop below ~120k or tx reverts)
  gasLimitBatch:  200000

--- DEPLOYMENT STATUS ---

Contract is deployed ONCE per network; reuse the same address everywhere (dev/demo/prod).

  Network:          Polygon Amoy testnet (chainId 80002)
  Contract address: 0x8CAeedf1dF7EE5119D18630EfC271C30cbc3909A
  Anchoring wallet: 0x6B9202c868A92f6Ac2d347D0064311E964964B21 (authorized)
  Explorer:         https://amoy.polygonscan.com/address/0x8CAeedf1dF7EE5119D18630EfC271C30cbc3909A
  Verified end-to-end: anchor + verify(match) + verify(tampered→false) confirmed on-chain.

Deploy toolchain — ETHERS.JS (not Hardhat) — see §60:
  node scripts/deploy-ethers.cjs        (deploy + authorizeAnchor + sync address; ethers v6 only)
  The contract is already compiled — bytecode + ABI live in contracts/DocLoqAnchor.json, so
  deployment needs NO hardhat. Hardhat remains available ONLY to (re)compile/test the .sol if it
  changes (`npx hardhat compile` / `npx hardhat test`); the runtime + deploy path use ethers.js.

Deploy to a server (demo/prod) — NO redeploy needed:
  - Carry the 5 blockchain vars in backend/.env (set manually / secret manager; .env is gitignored)
  - Carry contracts/DocLoqAnchor.json (ABI + address; committed to repo)
  - Use the SAME anchoring wallet (only authorized wallets can write; others are rejected)
  - Keep the wallet funded with POL — each anchor costs gas (~0.009 POL when gas is high).
    Testnet POL from https://faucet.polygon.technology
  - Redeploy ONLY if DocLoqAnchor.sol changes or moving to mainnet (chainId 137 → new address)
