================================================================================
17. INFRASTRUCTURE MIGRATION: MINIO -> CLOUDFLARE R2 + VAULT HA
================================================================================

Migration Date: April 2026

--- OVERVIEW ---

DocLoq migrated from self-hosted MinIO to Cloudflare R2 for object storage,
and added HashiCorp Vault HA (Raft storage backend) for production key
management. Cloudflare Tunnel replaces traditional port exposure for secure
ingress in production.

--- STORAGE MIGRATION (MinIO -> Cloudflare R2) ---

What changed:
  1. minio.service.js renamed to s3.service.js
     - Updated log prefixes from [MinIO] to [S3]
     - Fixed CopySource format for R2 compatibility (leading slash required)
     - Capped presigned URL expiry to R2 max of 7 days (604800s)

  2. storage.service.js updated
     - Import path changed from ./minio.service.js to ./s3.service.js
     - Provider checks now use isS3Provider helper (supports both 'minio' and 'r2')

  3. archive.service.js updated
     - Same import path and provider check updates

  4. upload.config.js updated
     - STORAGE_PROVIDER now accepts: local | minio | r2
     - Default S3_REGION changed from 'us-east-1' to 'auto' (R2 uses 'auto')

  5. docker-compose.yml updated
     - Removed minio and minio-init services
     - Removed minio_data volume
     - R2 is a cloud service, no local container needed

  6. .env updated
     - STORAGE_PROVIDER=r2
     - S3_ENDPOINT=https://<ACCOUNT_ID>.r2.cloudflarestorage.com
     - S3_REGION=auto

What did NOT change:
  - All S3 operations still use @aws-sdk/client-s3 (R2 is S3-compatible)
  - Envelope encryption flow is completely unchanged
  - The 15-step upload pipeline is unchanged
  - Frontend has zero changes (no direct S3 access)
  - Database schema unchanged (s3Key, s3Bucket fields still used)

R2 Buckets:
  - docloq-documents   : Encrypted document ciphertext + .meta.json sidecars
  - docloq-archive     : Archived document versions (GDPR retention)
  - docloq-qrcodes     : QR code verification PNG images

--- VAULT HIGH AVAILABILITY (Raft Storage Backend) ---

Production: 3-node Vault cluster with Raft integrated storage.
Development: Single dev-mode Vault (unchanged, in-memory).

Config files: backend/vault/config/vault-{1,2,3}.hcl

Raft HA features:
  - Automatic leader election among 3 nodes
  - Auto retry_join for cluster formation
  - No external Consul dependency
  - TLS disabled at Vault level (terminated at Cloudflare Tunnel)

Initialization (one-time):
  vault operator init -key-shares=3 -key-threshold=2
  # Unseal each node with 2 of 3 keys
  # Enable Transit engine + create docloq-master key

Backup:
  vault operator raft snapshot save /backups/vault-YYYYMMDD.snap

Docker Compose (production overlay):
  docker compose -f docker-compose.yml -f docker-compose.prod.yml up -d

--- CLOUDFLARE SERVICES ---

1. Cloudflare Tunnel (cloudflared)
   - Secure ingress without public port exposure
   - Routes api.docloq.site -> backend:3000
   - Configured in docker-compose.prod.yml

2. Cloudflare WAF (recommended)
   - Rate limiting: login 5 req/min, general 100 req/min per IP
   - OWASP Core Ruleset enabled
   - Content-Length limit > 55MB blocked

3. Cloudflare DDoS Protection
   - Automatic L3/L4 DDoS protection
   - Bot Fight Mode for frontend

4. Trust Proxy
   - backend/src/index.js: app.set('trust proxy', 1) in production
   - Ensures express-rate-limit reads real client IP from CF-Connecting-IP

--- FILES CHANGED ---

Renamed:
  backend/src/services/minio.service.js -> backend/src/services/s3.service.js

Modified:
  backend/src/services/s3.service.js         CopySource fix, log prefixes, presigned URL cap
  backend/src/services/storage.service.js    Import path, isS3Provider helper
  backend/src/services/archive.service.js    Import path, isS3Provider checks
  backend/src/config/upload.config.js        R2 region default, provider comment
  backend/docker-compose.yml                 Removed MinIO services
  backend/src/index.js                       Trust proxy for Cloudflare
  backend/.env.example                       Full R2 + Vault HA + Cloudflare config

New:
  backend/vault/config/vault-1.hcl           Vault HA node 1 config (Raft)
  backend/vault/config/vault-2.hcl           Vault HA node 2 config (Raft)
  backend/vault/config/vault-3.hcl           Vault HA node 3 config (Raft)
  backend/docker-compose.prod.yml            Production overlay (Vault HA + cloudflared)

================================================================================
18. DOCKER SWARM VPS DEPLOYMENT
================================================================================

DocLoq is fully containerized and orchestrated via Docker Swarm on a single VPS.
All services run as Docker containers with automatic restart, health checks,
rolling updates, and zero-downtime deployments.

18.1 RECOMMENDED VPS SPECS
--------------------------

  CPU    : 4 vCPUs minimum (8 recommended — ClamAV + OnlyOffice are heavy)
  RAM    : 16 GB minimum
  Disk   : 100 GB SSD
  OS     : Ubuntu 24.04 LTS
  Total peak RAM: ~11 GB across 14 services

18.2 ARCHITECTURE OVERVIEW
--------------------------

  Internet
     |
  [Cloudflare Edge — TLS termination, DDoS, CDN]
     |
  [cloudflared tunnel — outbound-only connection]
     |
  [Docker Swarm Overlay Network]
     |
     +---> frontend (Nginx, 2 replicas)
     +---> backend (Node.js, 2 replicas)
     +---> db (PostgreSQL 16)
     +---> mongodb (Mongo 7.0)
     +---> vault-1, vault-2, vault-3 (HA Raft cluster)
     +---> clamav (virus scanning)
     +---> qdrant (vector search)
     +---> onlyoffice (document editor)
     +---> docuseal (e-signing)
     +---> gitea + gitea-db (Git server)
     +---> cloudflared (tunnel ingress)

  Total: 14 services in a single Docker Swarm stack

18.3 SERVICE INVENTORY
----------------------

  Service       | Image                       | Replicas | Mem Limit | Purpose
  --------------|-----------------------------|---------:|----------:|--------
  backend       | docloq-backend:latest       |        2 |      1 GB | API server
  frontend      | docloq-frontend:latest      |        2 |    128 MB | Nginx SPA
  db            | postgres:16-alpine          |        1 |      1 GB | App database
  mongodb       | mongo:7.0                   |        1 |    512 MB | AI cache
  vault-1       | hashicorp/vault:1.17        |        1 |    512 MB | Key mgmt (leader)
  vault-2       | hashicorp/vault:1.17        |        1 |    512 MB | Key mgmt (follower)
  vault-3       | hashicorp/vault:1.17        |        1 |    512 MB | Key mgmt (follower)
  clamav        | clamav/clamav:stable        |        1 |      2 GB | Virus scanner
  qdrant        | qdrant/qdrant:v1.13.1       |        1 |      1 GB | Vector DB
  onlyoffice    | onlyoffice/documentserver   |        1 |      3 GB | Doc editor
  docuseal      | docuseal/docuseal:latest    |        1 |    512 MB | E-signing
  gitea         | gitea/gitea:latest          |        1 |    512 MB | Git server
  gitea-db      | postgres:16-alpine          |        1 |    256 MB | Gitea database
  cloudflared   | cloudflare/cloudflared      |        1 |    128 MB | Tunnel ingress

18.4 VPS INITIAL SETUP
-----------------------

  Step 1: Create deploy user + SSH hardening
    adduser deploy && usermod -aG sudo deploy
    # /etc/ssh/sshd_config: Port 2222, PermitRootLogin no, PasswordAuthentication no

  Step 2: Firewall (UFW)
    ufw default deny incoming
    ufw default allow outgoing
    ufw allow 2222/tcp    # SSH only — NO 80/443 needed (Cloudflare Tunnel)
    ufw enable

  Step 3: Swap space (4 GB)
    fallocate -l 4G /swapfile
    chmod 600 /swapfile && mkswap /swapfile && swapon /swapfile
    echo '/swapfile none swap sw 0 0' >> /etc/fstab

  Step 4: Install Docker + Init Swarm
    curl -fsSL https://get.docker.com | sh
    usermod -aG docker deploy
    docker swarm init --advertise-addr <VPS_PUBLIC_IP>

  Step 5: Directory structure
    /opt/docloq/
    ├── repo/          Git clone of the project
    ├── secrets/       Secret source files (deleted after setup)
    ├── backups/       Backup output
    └── scripts/       Maintenance scripts

18.5 DOCKER IMAGES
------------------

  Backend (backend/Dockerfile):
    - Multi-stage build on node:22-alpine
    - Stage 1 (deps): Install production npm dependencies
      * python3, make, g++ for native modules
      * Sharp uses prebuilt binaries (no vips-dev needed)
    - Stage 2 (runtime): Copy node_modules, app source, entrypoint
      * Runs as non-root user "docloq"
      * Uses tini as PID 1 init process
      * Health check: curl http://localhost:3000/
    - Final size: ~1.26 GB

  Frontend (frontend/Dockerfile):
    - Multi-stage build
    - Stage 1: npm ci + vite build (with VITE_* build args)
    - Stage 2: nginx:1.27-alpine serving static dist/
      * SPA fallback (try_files -> /index.html)
      * Static asset caching (1 year, immutable)
      * Gzip compression
      * /health endpoint for Swarm health checks
    - Final size: ~84 MB

18.6 ENTRYPOINT & SECRETS
--------------------------

  Backend uses entrypoint.sh to load Docker Swarm secrets at runtime:

    1. Reads all files from /run/secrets/
    2. Exports each as uppercase environment variable
       (e.g., /run/secrets/db_password -> DB_PASSWORD)
    3. Optionally runs database migrations (RUN_MIGRATIONS=true)
    4. Executes the CMD (node src/index.js)

  Docker Swarm Secrets (13 total):
    db_password, jwt_secret, encryption_master_key, vault_token,
    s3_access_key, s3_secret_key, openai_api_key, mongo_password,
    mail_password, qr_signing_secret, docuseal_api_key,
    cloudflare_tunnel_token, gitea_db_password

  Created via: echo "value" | docker secret create secret_name -
  Or from files: docker secret create secret_name /opt/docloq/secrets/secret_name

18.7 CLOUDFLARE TUNNEL ROUTING
-------------------------------

  Single tunnel, multiple public hostname routes:

    Public Hostname        -> Internal Service
    docloq.site            -> http://frontend:80
    api.docloq.site        -> http://backend:3000
    git.docloq.site        -> http://gitea:3000
    office.docloq.site     -> http://onlyoffice:80
    sign.docloq.site       -> http://docuseal:3000

  All internal traffic is HTTP (TLS terminated at Cloudflare edge).
  No ports exposed on VPS — cloudflared connects outbound only.

18.8 SWARM ORCHESTRATION
-------------------------

  Rolling Updates (backend + frontend):
    parallelism: 1
    delay: 30s
    failure_action: rollback
    order: start-first          (new container starts before old stops)

  Restart Policy (all services):
    condition: any
    delay: 5s
    max_attempts: 5
    window: 120s

  Health Checks (all services):
    - backend:    curl http://localhost:3000/
    - frontend:   wget http://localhost/health
    - db:         pg_isready -U docloq
    - mongodb:    mongosh --eval db.adminCommand('ping')
    - qdrant:     wget http://localhost:6333/healthz
    - onlyoffice: curl http://localhost/healthcheck
    - clamav:     clamdcheck
    - gitea:      wget http://localhost:3000/api/v1/version

  Placement Constraints:
    Stateful services (db, mongodb, qdrant, vault-1) pinned to manager node

18.9 GITEA GIT SERVER
----------------------

  Purpose: Self-hosted Git at git.docloq.site for team code management
  Access:  HTTPS-only through Cloudflare Tunnel (no SSH)

  Configuration:
    - Dedicated PostgreSQL database (gitea-db service)
    - Registration disabled (GITEA__service__DISABLE_REGISTRATION=true)
    - Signin required to view repos (GITEA__service__REQUIRE_SIGNIN_VIEW=true)
    - SSH disabled (all Git operations via HTTPS through tunnel)

  Note: Gitea does NOT support native HA clustering. Single replica with
  Swarm auto-restart provides sufficient uptime for small teams. Nightly
  backups of gitea_data volume + gitea-db PostgreSQL ensure recoverability.

18.10 DEPLOYMENT WORKFLOW
--------------------------

  scripts/deploy.sh supports these modes:

    ./scripts/deploy.sh --init      Full first-time setup:
                                      1. Create Docker secrets from files
                                      2. Create Docker configs (Vault HCL)
                                      3. Build backend + frontend images
                                      4. Deploy stack

    ./scripts/deploy.sh --build     Rebuild images + redeploy

    ./scripts/deploy.sh --secrets   Create/update secrets + configs only

    ./scripts/deploy.sh             Deploy/update stack only (no rebuild)

  Post-init steps:
    1. Initialize Vault:  vault operator init
    2. Unseal all 3 nodes (2 keys each)
    3. Enable Transit:    vault secrets enable transit
    4. Create key:        vault write transit/keys/docloq-master type=aes256-gcm96
    5. Configure Cloudflare Tunnel routes in Zero Trust dashboard
    6. Set up backup cron

18.11 BACKUP STRATEGY
----------------------

  scripts/backup.sh runs via cron at 03:00 daily:
    0 3 * * * /opt/docloq/scripts/backup.sh >> /opt/docloq/backups/backup.log 2>&1

  What gets backed up:
    1. PostgreSQL (app database)     -> pg_app_YYYYMMDD_HHMM.sql.gz
    2. PostgreSQL (Gitea database)   -> pg_gitea_YYYYMMDD_HHMM.sql.gz
    3. Vault Raft snapshot           -> vault_YYYYMMDD_HHMM.snap
    4. MongoDB (AI cache)            -> mongo_YYYYMMDD_HHMM.archive.gz

  Retention: 7 days (automatic cleanup of older backups)
  Documents: Stored in Cloudflare R2 (cloud-managed, not backed up locally)

18.12 DATABASE MIGRATIONS
--------------------------

  Drizzle ORM migrations run via entrypoint.sh on container startup:

    - Only the first backend replica has RUN_MIGRATIONS=true
    - Runs: npx drizzle-kit migrate
    - Second replica starts without migration (prevents race conditions)
    - Migration files stored in drizzle/ directory, committed to Git

18.13 LOGGING
-------------

  All services use json-file driver with rotation:
    max-size: 10 MB per log file
    max-file: 3 files per service

  View logs:
    docker service logs docloq_backend --follow
    docker service logs docloq_frontend --follow --tail 100

18.14 NETWORK
-------------

  Single overlay network: docloq_overlay
    - driver: overlay
    - attachable: true
    - All 14 services connected
    - Internal DNS: services reachable by name (e.g., http://db:5432)

  No ports published on host — all external access via Cloudflare Tunnel.

18.15 VOLUMES (13 NAMED VOLUMES)
---------------------------------

  postgres_data     PostgreSQL app database
  mongodb_data      MongoDB AI cache
  vault_data_1      Vault node 1 Raft storage
  vault_data_2      Vault node 2 Raft storage
  vault_data_3      Vault node 3 Raft storage
  clamav_data       ClamAV virus definitions
  qdrant_data       Qdrant vector storage
  onlyoffice_data   OnlyOffice document data
  onlyoffice_logs   OnlyOffice logs
  onlyoffice_lib    OnlyOffice libraries
  docuseal_data     DocuSeal signing data
  gitea_data        Gitea repository data
  gitea_db_data     Gitea PostgreSQL data

--- FILES CREATED ---

  backend/Dockerfile              Multi-stage Node.js 22 Alpine build
  backend/entrypoint.sh           Secret loading + optional migration
  backend/.dockerignore           Exclude .env, node_modules, storage/
  frontend/Dockerfile             Multi-stage Vite build -> Nginx serve
  frontend/nginx.conf             SPA routing, caching, gzip, health check
  frontend/.dockerignore          Exclude node_modules, dist
  docker-stack.yml                Unified Docker Swarm stack (14 services)
  scripts/deploy.sh               Build + deploy helper (--init, --build, --secrets)
  scripts/backup.sh               Daily backup cron (PostgreSQL, Vault, MongoDB)

================================================================================
20. KNOWN ISSUES & BUG FIXES (2026-04-18)
================================================================================

--- FIXED ---

FIX-001: req.user.id undefined in optionalAuth middleware
  File:  backend/src/middlewares/auth.middleware.js
  Root:  JWT payload stores userId but controllers read req.user?.id.
         optionalAuth skips session-table check, so id was never normalized.
  Fix:   Both authenticate and optionalAuth now do:
           req.user = { ...decoded, id: decoded.userId }
  Impact: Fixed org mismatch in upload, AI analysis, download watermark.
           Documents now correctly scoped to uploading user's organization.

FIX-002: POST /api/folders/move-folder → HTTP 500 on invalid UUID
  File:  backend/src/controllers/folder.controller.js
  Root:  folderId and newParentId passed directly to Postgres without UUID format validation.
  Fix:   UUID regex check before DB query; returns HTTP 400 with clear message.

FIX-003: GET /api/signing/:taskId/status → HTTP 500 on non-UUID taskId
  File:  backend/src/controllers/signing.controller.js
  Root:  taskId passed directly to Postgres WHERE clause without UUID validation.
  Fix:   UUID regex check before DB query; returns HTTP 400 with clear message.

FIX-004: Chatbot crash (HTTP 500) when user has no organizationId
  File:  backend/src/services/chatbot.service.js
  Root:  getOrCreateSession() passed null organizationId to DB INSERT which has
         NOT NULL constraint on chat_sessions.organization_id.
  Fix:   Early null check throws descriptive error before attempting INSERT.

--- DOCUMENTATION CORRECTIONS ---

DOC-001: POST /api/auth/register — field name for org creation
  Correct field: companyName  (NOT orgName)
  Body: { email, password, firstName, lastName, companyName? }
  First user of an org is automatically assigned 'admin' role.

DOC-002: PATCH /api/notifications/read-all (NOT POST)
  Route: router.patch('/read-all', readAll)  — HTTP method is PATCH.

DOC-003: Watermark Scanner / OSINT is OWNER-only by design
  Roles allowed: owner   (watermark-scanner.routes + osint.routes use authorize(['owner']))
  Roles blocked:  admin, user
  Rationale: Leak detection and download history are sensitive security
             operations restricted to the tenant owner. (Updated §42 — was the old
             super_admin/admin/manager/auditor model.)

DOC-004: Document Verification (QR) is OWNER-ONLY  [SUPERSEDED — see §66.C]
  GET /api/documents/verify?code=XXXXXX — authenticate + authorize(['owner']).
  This entry previously claimed the endpoint was public by design. That is no
  longer true and the code no longer allows it: an unauthenticated QR scan is
  rejected. The public-scan rationale was dropped because the endpoint returned
  document name, uploader and upload date to anyone holding a printed code,
  which leaks tenant metadata to a party outside the organization.

================================================================================
21. SECURITY AUDIT & HARDENING (2026-04-19)
================================================================================

A comprehensive security audit identified and fixed the following issues:

21.1 UUID VALIDATION MIDDLEWARE
--------------------------------

Problem: All controllers accepted :id params from URL and passed them directly
to PostgreSQL without format validation. A non-UUID string caused Postgres to
crash with "invalid input syntax for type uuid" → HTTP 500.

Fix: Created backend/src/middlewares/validate.middleware.js

  const UUID_RE = /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i;

  export const validateUUID = (...paramNames) => (req, res, next) => {
    for (const param of paramNames) {
      const val = req.params[param];
      if (val && !UUID_RE.test(val)) {
        return res.status(400).json({ success: false, message: `Invalid ${param} format` });
      }
    }
    next();
  };

Applied to 40+ endpoints across 13 route files:
  document.routes.js, folder.routes.js, task.routes.js, notification.routes.js,
  ai-analysis.routes.js, user.routes.js, watermark-scanner.routes.js,
  blockchain.routes.js, form.routes.js, department.routes.js, trash.routes.js,
  signing.routes.js, role.routes.js

Result: Invalid UUID → HTTP 400 (not 500)

21.2 DEV FALLBACK REMOVAL
--------------------------

Problem: 5 controllers contained a fallback block: "if user has no orgId, fetch
first org from DB." This meant if auth middleware was bypassed or token was
invalid, the request was processed with another org's data — a critical
authorization bypass.

Fix: Removed all dev fallback blocks. Replaced with proper 401:
  if (!orgId) return res.status(401).json({ success: false, message: 'Unauthorized' });

Affected controllers:
  folder.controller.js   — getAllFolders, createFolder
  document.controller.js — uploadDocument, uploadDocumentSimple
  form.controller.js     — resolveOrgId() helper removed, replaced inline
  trash.controller.js    — resolveOrgId() helper removed
  dashboard.controller.js — getDashboardStats

21.3 UPLOAD ENDPOINT AUTH FIX
-------------------------------

Problem: POST /api/documents/upload used optionalAuth middleware, meaning
anonymous requests were accepted. Combined with dev fallback, any unauthenticated
user could upload documents.

Fix: Changed to authenticate (required):
  router.post('/upload', authenticate, upload.single('file'), uploadDocument);
  router.post('/upload-simple', authenticate, upload.single('file'), uploadDocumentSimple);

Note: GET /:id/file and GET /:id/download remain optionalAuth intentionally —
OnlyOffice fetches them with a signed token rather than a user JWT. The verification
endpoints are NOT in that group: they are owner-only (see §66.C).

21.4 BLOCKCHAIN ROUTER-LEVEL GUARD
------------------------------------

Problem: BLOCKCHAIN_ENABLED=false only disabled functionality inside controllers.
Routes were still accessible, and one endpoint (updateWallet) leaked internal
config: RPC URL, contract address, chain ID.

Fix: Added 503 guard at router middleware level in blockchain.routes.js:
  router.use((req, res, next) => {
    if (!uploadConfig.blockchain.enabled)
      return res.status(503).json({ success: false, message: 'Blockchain service not enabled' });
    next();
  });

Also removed config leak from updateWalletHandler response.
Result: All /api/blockchain/* routes return 503 when disabled.

21.5 REDIS-BACKED RATE LIMITER
--------------------------------

Problem: express-rate-limit with in-memory store resets on every restart and
does not work correctly in multi-instance deployments.

Fix: Replaced with rate-limiter-flexible backed by Redis (ioredis):
  - Global limiter: 200 requests per 15 minutes per IP
  - Auth limiter:   15 requests per 15 minutes per IP (brute-force protection)
  - Fallback: in-memory RateLimiterMemory if Redis is unreachable
  - Redis service added to docker-compose.yml (dev) and docker-stack.yml (prod)

New env var: REDIS_URL=redis://redis:6379

21.6 FORM & FOLDER ROUTES AUTH FIX
-------------------------------------

Problem: form.routes.js and folder.routes.js were missing router.use(authenticate)
at the top level. Individual routes had authenticate on some routes but not all,
leaving some endpoints unprotected.

Fix: Added router.use(authenticate) at the top of both route files. All form
and folder endpoints now require authentication.

22. MULTI-REPO GIT ARCHITECTURE
================================================================================

The project was split from a monorepo into 3 independent Git repositories,
each deployed separately and maintained independently.

22.1 REPOSITORY STRUCTURE
---------------------------

  git.docloq.site/docloq/backend   — Express API + all services + migrations
  git.docloq.site/docloq/frontend  — React 19 + Vite + Nginx
  git.docloq.site/docloq/infra     — Docker configs + scripts + deployment

22.2 BACKEND REPO CONTENTS
----------------------------

  src/                     Express application source
    controllers/           Request handlers (17 controllers)
    services/              Business logic (24 services)
    routes/                Route definitions (14 route files)
    middlewares/           Auth, validate, admin, permission
    db/                    Drizzle schema + connection
    config/                upload.config.js, auth.config.js
  drizzle/                 SQL migration files (0000-0007)
  vault/config/            Vault HCL files (vault-1.hcl, vault-2.hcl, vault-3.hcl)
  Dockerfile               Multi-stage Node.js 22 Alpine build
  entrypoint.sh            Secret loading + migration runner
  docker-compose.yml       Dev services (postgres, redis, vault, clamav, etc.)
  docker-compose.prod.yml  Prod overlay (HA vault cluster)
  .env.example             All environment variable documentation

22.3 FRONTEND REPO CONTENTS
-----------------------------

  src/
    features/              Page-level components (15 features)
    components/            Shared UI components
    services/              API service layer (18 services)
    app/                   Router, store, providers, guards
  Dockerfile               Multi-stage Vite → Nginx build
  nginx.conf               SPA routing, gzip, caching, health check
  .env.example             VITE_* variable documentation

--------------------------

  docker-stack.yml         Docker Swarm production stack (15 services)

    deploy.sh              Build + deploy helper script
    backup.sh              Daily backup cron script
  docs/                    Workplan documents
  .env.example             Complete VPS environment variable template
  README.md                Full VPS deployment guide

22.5 GITIGNORE STRATEGY
-------------------------

  Backend:  Excludes .env, node_modules/, storage/, drizzle/meta/, vault/data/
  Frontend: Excludes .env, node_modules/, dist/
  Infra:    Excludes docuseal/data/, onlyoffice/data|logs|lib/, secrets/

================================================================================
23. VPS PRODUCTION DEPLOYMENT (TRAEFIK)
================================================================================

Production deployment uses docker-compose.vps.yml from the infra repo,
with Traefik as reverse proxy (existing setup on VPS).

23.1 ARCHITECTURE
------------------

  Internet → Traefik (existing, "web" network) → DocLoq containers
                                                → Gitea
                                                → Adminer

  DocLoq internal services communicate via "internal" bridge network
  (postgres, redis, mongodb, qdrant, vault, clamav — not exposed to Traefik)

23.2 DOCKER NETWORKS
---------------------

  web       (external) — Traefik proxy network; backend, frontend,
                         onlyoffice, docuseal join this network
  internal  (internal) — DocLoq-only; databases, vault, clamav, qdrant,
                         redis — no external access

23.3 VPS SERVICES (docker-compose.vps.yml)
-------------------------------------------

+------------------+------------------------------+----------------------------+
| Service          | Image                        | Notes                      |
+------------------+------------------------------+----------------------------+
| backend          | docloq-backend:latest        | Built from backend repo    |
| frontend         | docloq-frontend:latest       | Built from frontend repo   |
| postgres         | postgres:16-alpine           | Primary DB, internal only  |
| redis            | redis:7-alpine               | Rate limiter, internal     |
| mongodb          | mongo:7.0                    | AI cache, internal         |
| qdrant           | qdrant/qdrant:v1.13.1        | Vector DB, internal        |
| vault            | hashicorp/vault:1.17         | Single-node, internal      |
| clamav           | clamav/clamav:stable         | Malware scanner, internal  |
| onlyoffice       | onlyoffice/documentserver    | Document editor            |
| docuseal         | docuseal/docuseal:latest     | E-signature service        |
+------------------+------------------------------+----------------------------+

23.4 TRAEFIK LABELS (per service)
-----------------------------------

  Backend labels example:
    traefik.enable=true
    traefik.docker.network=web
    traefik.http.routers.docloq-api.rule=Host(`api.docloq.site`)
    traefik.http.routers.docloq-api.entrypoints=web
    traefik.http.services.docloq-api.loadbalancer.server.port=3000

  HTTPS: uncomment websecure router + tls.certresolver=letsencrypt in compose

23.5 DOMAIN MAP
----------------

  docloq.site          → frontend (port 80)
  api.docloq.site      → backend  (port 3000)
  office.docloq.site   → onlyoffice (port 80)
  sign.docloq.site     → docuseal   (port 3000)

23.6 ENVIRONMENT CONSTRUCTION
-------------------------------

Sensitive URLs (DATABASE_URL, MONGO_URL) are constructed dynamically in
entrypoint.sh after loading Docker secrets:

  if [ -n "$DB_PASSWORD" ] && [ -z "$DATABASE_URL" ]; then
    export DATABASE_URL="postgresql://docloq:${DB_PASSWORD}@db:5432/docloq_db"
  fi

  if [ -n "$MONGO_PASSWORD" ] && [ -z "$MONGO_URL" ]; then
    export MONGO_URL="mongodb://docloq:${MONGO_PASSWORD}@mongodb:27017/docloq_ai_cache?authSource=admin"
  fi

23.7 VAULT INITIALIZATION (once per deployment)
-------------------------------------------------

  docker exec -it <vault-container> sh
  vault operator init -key-shares=3 -key-threshold=2
  # Save 3 unseal keys + root token securely
  vault operator unseal <key1>
  vault operator unseal <key2>
  vault login <root-token>
  vault secrets enable transit
  vault write -f transit/keys/docloq-master

  Note: Vault must be manually unsealed after every VPS reboot.
  Production should use auto-unseal via cloud KMS or HSM.

23.8 VOLUMES (10 NAMED VOLUMES)
---------------------------------

  postgres_data   redis_data   mongodb_data   qdrant_data   vault_data
  clamav_data     onlyoffice_data   onlyoffice_logs   onlyoffice_lib   docuseal_data

================================================================================
24. CI/CD PIPELINE (GITEA ACTIONS)
================================================================================

Automated build and deploy using Gitea Actions with self-hosted runner on VPS.

24.1 FLOW
----------

  Developer pushes to main branch
       ↓
  Gitea receives push → triggers workflow
       ↓
  Self-hosted runner on VPS receives job
       ↓
  docker build -t docloq-backend:latest .  (or frontend)
       ↓
  docker compose -f /opt/docloq/infra/docker-compose.vps.yml up -d --no-deps
       ↓
  Health check curl http://localhost:3000/
       ↓
  ✅ Deploy complete (zero downtime — container replaced)

24.2 WORKFLOW FILES
--------------------

  backend/.gitea/workflows/deploy.yml
  frontend/.gitea/workflows/deploy.yml

  Backend workflow:
    - Trigger: push to main
    - runs-on: self-hosted
    - Steps: checkout → docker build → docker compose up --no-deps → health check

  Frontend workflow:
    - Trigger: push to main
    - Build args: VITE_API_URL, VITE_ONLYOFFICE_URL
    - Steps: checkout → docker build (with --build-arg) → compose up → health check

24.3 RUNNER REQUIREMENTS
--------------------------

  - Gitea Runner registered as self-hosted on VPS
  - Runner container must mount:
      /var/run/docker.sock:/var/run/docker.sock   (docker access)
      /opt/docloq/infra:/opt/docloq/infra:ro      (compose file access)
  - Runner label: self-hosted

24.4 MANUAL UPDATE (no CI)
----------------------------

  cd /opt/docloq/backend && git pull && docker build -t docloq-backend:latest .
  docker compose -f /opt/docloq/infra/docker-compose.vps.yml up -d --no-deps --no-build backend

26. ONLYOFFICE COLLABORATION UPGRADE (2026-05-14)
================================================================================

OnlyOffice integration di-upgrade besar untuk pengalaman edit yang nyaman bagi
user awam: HTTPS fix, single-editor lock 2 jam, live presence, threaded comments
dengan @mention, dan UX polish (skeleton loader, breadcrumb, FAB save & close).

26.1 INFRA FIX (Traefik HTTPS untuk OnlyOffice)
------------------------------------------------

  Problem  : office.docloq.site tidak HTTPS — frontend (HTTPS) di-block mixed content.
             Label compose pakai certresolver=letsencrypt tapi Traefik define `le`.
  Fix      : infra/docker-compose.vps.yml
             - Aktifkan label HTTPS (websecure entrypoint + tls.certresolver=le)
             - Tambah middleware office-headers inject X-Forwarded-Proto=https
               agar OnlyOffice generate self-URL pakai HTTPS, bukan HTTP
             - Aktifkan HTTPS labels juga untuk backend, frontend (sync state server)

26.2 BACKEND ENCODING FIX (TXT BOM)
------------------------------------

  Problem : OnlyOffice prompt "pilih encoding" tiap kali buka file .txt/.csv.
  Fix     : backend/src/controllers/document.controller.js → serveDocument
            - Helper prependUtf8BomIfText(buffer, filename)
            - Inject BOM UTF-8 (0xEF 0xBB 0xBF) di awal stream untuk .txt/.csv
            - OnlyOffice auto-detect UTF-8 → skip prompt

26.3 PER-VERSION OnlyOffice KEY (paksa refetch)
-----------------------------------------------

  document.key = `${docId}-v${versionCount}-${updatedAt.getTime()}`
  Setiap versi baru → key baru → OnlyOffice fetch file lagi, tidak pakai cache lama.

26.4 EDIT LOCK (2-JAM ABSOLUT, SINGLE EDITOR)
----------------------------------------------

  Schema     : documents.editLockedBy, editLockAcquiredAt, editLockExpiresAt
  Index      : doc_lock_expires_idx ON editLockExpiresAt
  Service    : backend/src/services/document-lock.service.js
    - acquireEditLock(documentId, userId)
        TX SELECT FOR UPDATE → kalau no lock OR expired OR same user → set 2h lock
        Kalau locked user lain → return acquired=false + lockedByName + expiresAt
    - releaseEditLock(documentId, userId) — clear hanya jika owner match
    - forceReleaseLock(documentId) — admin override / save callback
  Behavior   :
    - getOnlyOfficeConfig dengan mode=edit → acquireEditLock dulu.
    - Kalau gagal acquire → response config mode=view + lockInfo {lockedByName, expiresAt}.
    - Frontend tampil banner kuning "Sedang diedit oleh {nama} sampai {jam}".
    - 2 jam absolut, no extension. Tepat saat habis → force-eject ke view mode.
    - Lock release otomatis di OnlyOffice callback status 2 (save+close) / status 4 (close no changes).
    - sendBeacon release di window.beforeunload (tab close).

26.5 LIVE PRESENCE (POLLING 10 DETIK)
--------------------------------------

  Schema  : documentPresence (id, documentId, userId, mode, lastHeartbeatAt)
  Service : backend/src/services/document-presence.service.js
    - heartbeat(documentId, userId, mode) — UPSERT ON CONFLICT (documentId, userId)
    - getPresence(documentId) — filter WHERE lastHeartbeatAt > now - 30s (stale masked)
    - leavePresence(documentId, userId)
  Behavior :
    - Frontend setInterval 10s: POST heartbeat + GET presence.
    - Header editor tampil avatar (max 3 + "+N more"), dot warna:
      hijau = sedang edit, biru = sedang lihat.
    - Stale rows tidak perlu sweeper — query filter mask.

26.6 THREADED COMMENTS + @MENTION
----------------------------------

  Schema   : documentComments (id, documentId, authorId, content,
             mentions JSONB, parentCommentId, editedAt, deletedAt, createdAt)
  Service  : backend/src/services/document-comment.service.js
    - createComment(documentId, authorId, content, mentions, parentCommentId)
      Fan-out notifikasi (dedupe author):
        * Mentioned users → notification.type = 'comment_mention'
        * Doc owner (jika bukan author dan tidak di-mention) → 'document_comment'
    - listComments(documentId) — threaded (parent + flat replies).
    - deleteComment(commentId, userId, userRole) — soft delete.
      Permitted: author OR role in ['owner','admin'] (document-comment.service.js:133).
    - searchMentionableUsers(documentId, query) — user di org yang sama, max 10.
  UI       : frontend/src/components/documents/CommentModal.jsx
    - Modal glassmorphism, sticky textarea bottom + send button.
    - @ trigger typeahead dropdown debounced 200ms.
    - Reply button per top-level comment → indented reply.
    - Cmd/Ctrl + Enter untuk kirim cepat.
    - Comment button di card document menggantikan Edit button (Edit tetap di
      Info modal → CTA "Edit in OnlyOffice").

26.7 EDITOR UX POLISH
----------------------

  File: frontend/src/components/onlyoffice/OnlyOfficeEditor.jsx (refactor besar)

  Fitur:
    a. Skeleton loader (OnlyOfficeSkeleton.jsx) — toolbar mock + page placeholder +
       progress bar 4 stage (script/config/init/ready), label Indonesia.
    b. Auto-recover — onError errorCode -1/-3/-4 → retry up to 3x silent
       fetch config + re-init. Indikator "Menyambung..." muncul saat retry.
    c. Theme sync — useTheme() hook → kirim ?theme=dark|light ke config endpoint.
       editorConfig.customization.uiTheme = "theme-dark" | "theme-classic-light".
       Toggle theme mid-session → auto-reload editor dengan tema baru.
    d. Bandwidth-aware — navigator.connection.effectiveType, slow-2g/2g → kirim
       ?bandwidth=slow → backend set config.type='mobile' (mode lite).
       Banner biru "Koneksi lambat terdeteksi" tampil.
    e. Reload button — toolbar header. Klik:
        * Ada perubahan belum tersimpan → toast warning "Save dulu".
        * Fetch fresh config → versionCount sama → toast "Sudah versi terbaru".
        * versionCount naik → destroy & re-init editor → toast "Dimuat versi vN".
    f. FAB Save & Close — fixed bottom-right (z-30). Edit mode + changes → hijau
       "Simpan & Tutup". Edit mode no changes / view mode → slate "Tutup".
       Spring animation in/out.
    g. Editable title — klik nama di header (edit mode) → input inline.
       Enter / blur → PATCH /:id rename. Escape → cancel.
    h. Breadcrumb — fetch /:id/breadcrumb on mount. Render Dokumen > Folder > nama.
       Walk folders.parentId chain max 12 level.
    i. Indonesian error map — semua errorCode OnlyOffice → pesan Indo.
    j. Glassmorphism shell — header backdrop-blur, smooth transitions 200ms.
    k. Save toast — di parent (Documents.jsx) — "Tersimpan v{n} · baru saja"
       muncul saat onSaved(versionNumber) callback fire dari editor.
    l. Force-eject UI — lock expired → confirm modal "Sesi 2 jam habis,
       kembali ke mode lihat" → auto save + release lock + reload as view.

26.8 OnlyOffice CONFIG HARDENING
---------------------------------

  editorConfig.customization tightened:
    - feedback: { visible: false }   (hilangkan feedback button)
    - goback:   { visible: false }   (hilangkan back-to-portal)
    - help: false                    (hide help menu)
    - compactToolbar: true           (toolbar compact mode)
    - hideRulers: true               (sembunyikan ruler)
    - uiTheme: theme-dark / theme-classic-light
  editorConfig.plugins: { autostart: [], pluginsData: [] }  (no plugins)
  editorConfig.lang: 'id'  (UI bahasa Indonesia)

26.9 NEW API ENDPOINTS (collab)
-------------------------------

  GET    /api/documents/:id/presence              [Auth] List active users
  POST   /api/documents/:id/presence              [Auth] Heartbeat (body: {mode})
  DELETE /api/documents/:id/presence              [Auth] Leave (or sendBeacon)
  POST   /api/documents/:id/edit-lock/release     [Auth] Release my lock
  GET    /api/documents/:id/breadcrumb            [Auth] Folder hierarchy
  PATCH  /api/documents/:id                       [Auth] Rename (body: {filename})
  GET    /api/documents/:id/comments              [Auth] Threaded comments
  POST   /api/documents/:id/comments              [Auth] Create comment
  DELETE /api/documents/comments/:commentId       [Auth] Delete (author/mod)
  GET    /api/documents/:id/mentionable-users     [Auth] Typeahead users (?q=)

26.10 MIGRATION
----------------

  File: backend/drizzle/0011_onlyoffice_collab.sql
    - ALTER documents ADD COLUMN edit_locked_by, edit_lock_acquired_at,
      edit_lock_expires_at + index
    - CREATE TABLE document_comments + 2 indexes
    - CREATE TABLE document_presence + unique idx + heartbeat idx

  Idempotent (IF NOT EXISTS). Run via drizzle-kit migrate atau psql manual.

26.11 KMS/VAULT FLOW
---------------------

  TIDAK BERUBAH. Semua flow envelope encryption tetap:
    Upload    → ClamAV → encrypt via Vault Transit per-doc key → R2
    Download  → R2 → Vault decrypt → per-download watermark → stream
    Edit save → OnlyOffice callback → upload pipeline → encrypt → new version
  JWT antara backend ↔ OnlyOffice = TIDAK aktif di phase ini (planned phase 2,
  butuh shared secret config).

================================================================================
27. EDIT ACCESS REQUEST PING + FORCE RELEASE (2026-05-14)
================================================================================

Iterasi lanjutan section 26 collaboration. Menambahkan kemampuan komunikasi
antar user saat dokumen di-lock, plus fix UX critical.

27.1 PING EDITOR (Request edit access)
---------------------------------------

  Endpoint: POST /api/documents/:id/ping-editor
  Pemanggil: user di mode view yang ingin minta giliran edit.
  Behavior:
    - Backend lookup documents.editLockedBy (user yang sedang edit)
    - Generate notification.type = 'edit_access_request' ke locker
      title: "{senderName} ingin mengedit {filename}"
      relatedType: 'document', relatedId: docId
    - Sender dapat toast "Notifikasi sudah dikirim."

  UI (frontend OnlyOfficeEditor.jsx):
    - Modal lock taken (klik tombol "Terkunci") tampil:
      * Avatar gradient + nama editor
      * Pill "Sesi berakhir dalam X menit"
      * 3 tombol: Minta akses edit / Paksa lepas (admin only) / Lihat saja
    - Saat user edit (mode=edit) terima notif edit_access_request:
      * Polling notification setiap 15s, filter by relatedId === documentId
      * Tampil floating card top-right (bawah header) selama 15 detik:
        - Avatar pemohon + "X ingin mengedit dokumen ini"
        - Tombol "Lepas akses": save → release lock → reload as view
        - Tombol "Nanti dulu": dismiss notif
        - Progress bar countdown 15s

27.2 FORCE RELEASE LOCK (Admin override)
-----------------------------------------

  Endpoint: POST /api/documents/:id/edit-lock/force-release
  Role guard: role in ['owner','admin'] (document-collab.controller.js:81).
  Behavior: clear edit_locked_by/edit_lock_acquired_at/edit_lock_expires_at
  unconditionally. Untuk recovery kalau lock orphan (user crash sebelum release).

  UI: tombol "Paksa lepas (Admin)" muncul di modal lock taken.

27.3 LOCK RELEASE RELIABILITY FIX
----------------------------------

  Problem: lock kadang stuck setelah user keluar tanpa save (close button).
  Sebab:
    - sendBeacon tidak kirim Authorization header → endpoint reject 401.
    - handleSaveAndClose hanya release lock saat ada perubahan + save sukses.

  Fix:
  1. authenticate middleware (backend/src/middlewares/auth.middleware.js):
     - Accept token via query param ?_t=<jwt> (selain Authorization header)
     - Khusus untuk sendBeacon yang tidak bisa kirim header
  2. handleSaveAndClose (frontend):
     - View mode → close langsung
     - Edit mode + no changes → release lock dulu lalu close
     - Edit mode + changes → save → poll versionCount → release lock → close
  3. beforeunload handler:
     - sendBeacon ke /presence + /edit-lock/release dengan ?_t=token query
     - Backup: fetch keepalive=true POST (kalau sendBeacon silent fail)

27.4 ONLYOFFICE TOOLBAR FIX
----------------------------

  Problem: tab bar OnlyOffice (File/Home/Insert/etc) tertutup header DocLoq.
  Sebab: editor container pakai top offset hardcoded 56px, tapi header DocLoq
  variable height (banner lock + breadcrumb bisa menambah tinggi).

  Fix: ResizeObserver di header DocLoq → state headerHeight dynamic → editor
  container top offset = headerHeight pixel. Header berubah → editor auto-shift.

27.5 TXT EDIT FIX
-----------------

  Problem: TXT file open di edit mode tapi tidak bisa typing.
  Fix di backend config:
    - editorConfig.coEditing = { mode: 'fast', change: mode === 'edit' }
    - permissions.edit eksplisit follow mode parameter
    - compactToolbar: false, hideRulers: false, compactHeader: false
      (sebelumnya true → toolbar edit tersembunyi)

-------------------------------

  POST /api/documents/:id/edit-lock/force-release  [Admin] Paksa lepas lock

29. CAPTCHA MIGRATION: hCAPTCHA -> CLOUDFLARE TURNSTILE (2026-05-24)
================================================================================

Konteks: hCaptcha invisible popup bermasalah (focus-steal saat submit form,
token expiry race condition, widget render flaky di dark theme). Diganti
penuh dengan Cloudflare Turnstile (gratis, privacy-first, mode managed
auto-detect invisible/checkbox). Google login flow `?recaptcha=` lama juga
diretire — semua captcha flow sekarang single token Turnstile.

29.1 NEW BEHAVIOR
------------------

  - Turnstile widget render visible (theme dark, size flexible) di Login form.
  - Sign in + Sign in with Google buttons keduanya DISABLED sampai captcha
    solve. Tooltip "Selesaikan captcha untuk login Google" muncul saat hover
    button Google yang masih disabled.
  - Token sekali pakai. Failed login auto-reset captcha widget supaya user
    dapat fresh token tanpa refresh halaman.
  - Backend dev mode (TURNSTILE_SECRET_KEY tidak diset) auto-bypass dengan
    warning log. Hindari di production (controller throws).

29.2 BACKEND CHANGES
---------------------

  Service: src/services/turnstile.service.js (NEW)
    - verifyTurnstile(token, remoteIp) → throw on fail
    - URL: https://challenges.cloudflare.com/turnstile/v0/siteverify
    - Form-urlencoded POST { secret, response, remoteip? }
    - Throws specific message dengan error-codes Cloudflare

  Controller: src/controllers/auth.controller.js
    - login() + register() terima { captchaToken } di body (rename dari
      `hcaptchaToken`)
    - Sebelum lookup user → verifyTurnstile(captchaToken, req.ip)
    - hCaptcha helper (verifyHcaptcha) DIHAPUS bersama config block-nya
      di auth.config.js

  Controller: src/controllers/google-oauth.controller.js
    - initiateGoogleLogin() ambil token dari ?captcha= (rename dari
      ?recaptcha=)
    - verifyTurnstile sebelum redirect ke Google consent
    - Service recaptcha.service.js DEPRECATED (file masih ada untuk
      backward-compat, tapi tidak dipakai code path manapun)

29.3 FRONTEND CHANGES
----------------------

  Package: @marsidev/react-turnstile ^1.5.2 (NEW)

  Komponen: src/features/auth/login.jsx
    - Import Turnstile dari @marsidev/react-turnstile
    - State captchaToken + ref turnstileRef
    - isSubmitDisabled = isLoading || authLoading || !captchaToken
    - isGoogleDisabled = googleLoading || !captchaToken
    - Google handler append ?captcha=<token> ke URL OAuth init
    - Pada gagal login: turnstileRef.current?.reset() + clear token

  Service: src/services/auth.service.js
    - login(email, password, captchaToken, rememberMe) — param ke-3 diganti
    - Body field {captchaToken} (tidak lagi hcaptchaToken)

  Hook: src/hooks/useRecaptcha.js — tetap ada tapi tidak dipakai.
  Lib: @hcaptcha/react-hcaptcha tetap di package.json (user request retain).

29.4 ENV VARS
--------------

  Tambah:
    TURNSTILE_SECRET_KEY=0x...                  # backend siteverify
    VITE_TURNSTILE_SITE_KEY=0x...               # frontend widget render

  Set ke false (deprecated, tidak dibaca lagi tapi tetap ada di .env):
    HCAPTCHA_ENABLED=false
    VITE_HCAPTCHA_ENABLED=false

  Test keys Cloudflare (always-pass / always-fail untuk dev):
    Site key always-pass:    1x00000000000000000000AA
    Secret key always-pass:  1x0000000000000000000000000000000AA

29.5 CI WORKFLOW UPDATE
------------------------

  frontend/.gitea/workflows/deploy.yml — tambah build-arg supaya
  VITE_TURNSTILE_SITE_KEY ter-baked ke static bundle:

    env:
      VITE_TURNSTILE_SITE_KEY: 0x4AAAAAADVVXAV9IObkcwD2

    docker build \
      --build-arg VITE_TURNSTILE_SITE_KEY=${{ env.VITE_TURNSTILE_SITE_KEY }} \
      ...

  frontend/Dockerfile — drop ARG VITE_HCAPTCHA_*, tambah ARG VITE_TURNSTILE_SITE_KEY.

================================================================================
30. DOCUMENT VERIFICATION WIRING (FE<->BE) (2026-05-24)
================================================================================

  >>> PARTIALLY SUPERSEDED BY §48 (2026-06-28). The verify-by-hash and verify-hardcopy
  >>> endpoints described below were REMOVED and replaced by POST /verify-file. The QR
  >>> short-code / QR-image wiring and verificationRequests logging still apply. Read §48
  >>> for the current verification contract; this section is kept as historical record.

Konteks: Tabel verificationRequests, endpoint QR shortCode, dan UI 3-step
Verification.jsx sudah ada lama, tapi tidak terhubung. Verification.jsx pakai
Math.random() + setTimeout untuk semua hasil. verificationRequests tidak
pernah di-INSERT. Document list tidak expose shortCode atau QR image URL.

Update ini meng-wire seluruh stack verification end-to-end, log setiap
percobaan ke verificationRequests, expose QR image lewat endpoint baru,
dan menambahkan dua method baru: QR image upload + hardcopy fuzzy
SimHash. SSDEEP tetap placeholder (no native compile), SimHash digunakan
sebagai fuzzy algorithm. [OUTDATED per 2026-06-30 — SSDEEP kini diimplementasi
pure-JS spamsum & aktif sebagai tie-breaker; lihat §8.5 + catatan §30.6.]

30.1 ARSITEKTUR FLOW
---------------------

  Frontend (Verification.jsx)
       |
       v
  [verification.service.js]  ── 5 method wrap REST endpoint
       |
       v
  Backend [document.controller.js]
       |
       +─> verifyByHash             (manual SHA-256 lookup)
       +─> verifyByShortCode        (owner-only QR shortcode lookup)
       +─> verifyByQrImage          (decode PNG/JPG → shortCode lookup)
       +─> verifyByHardcopy         (text extract → SimHash fuzzy match)
       +─> verifyDocument(:id)      (per-doc multi-method)
       +─> getDocumentQrImage(:id)  (serve QR PNG from R2)
       |
       v
  [verification.service.js (backend)]
       |
       +─> findByContentHash(sha256, orgId)
       +─> findBySimHash(simhashHex, orgId, limit)
       +─> decodeQrFromBuffer(imageBuffer)  ── jsqr + sharp
       +─> computeUploadDNA(fileBuffer, mimeType)
       +─> enrichDocument(doc)  ── uploader + latest blockchainAnchor
       |
       v
  [verificationRequests INSERT]  ── audit + analytics per percobaan

30.2 BACKEND NEW SERVICE
-------------------------

  src/services/verification.service.js (NEW)

  Function:
    findByContentHash(sha256, orgId=null)
      - Exact SHA-256 lookup (org-scoped jika user authenticated)

    findBySimHash(simhashHex, orgId=null, limit=5)
      - Query semua docs dengan simHash non-null
      - Hitung Hamming distance vs input
      - Threshold: distance ≤ 6 = verified, ≤ 14 = partial, lainnya skip
      - Return top N matches sorted by distance ASC

    decodeQrFromBuffer(imageBuffer)
      - sharp(image).ensureAlpha().raw() → RGBA
      - jsQR(rgba, width, height) → QR data string
      - Parse JSON { url, sig, payload } (format qrcode.service.js)
      - Extract shortCode dari payload.sc atau query string url
      - Return { shortCode, payload, signature, raw }

    computeUploadDNA(fileBuffer, mimeType)
      - sha256 = generateSHA256(buffer)
      - extractText via upload-pipeline.extractText (PDF, DOCX, TXT)
      - normalizeText → generateSimHash (16-char hex)
      - Return { sha256, simhash, hasText }

    enrichDocument(doc)
      - Lookup uploader dari users table by doc.ownerId
      - Lookup latest blockchainAnchor by documentId (order createdAt DESC)
      - Return { id, filename, mimeType, status, contentHash, simHash,
        createdAt, uploader: {id, name, email}, blockchainAnchor: {...} }

30.3 BACKEND CONTROLLER CHANGES
--------------------------------

  src/controllers/document.controller.js

  HELPER BARU (private): logVerificationRequest({...})
    - Insert ke verificationRequests dengan timestamp completedAt
    - Field: inputMethod, inputHash, inputQrCode, status,
      matchedDocumentId, simHashDistance, ssdeepSimilarity,
      blockchainVerified, blockchainAnchorId, requestedBy, ipAddress,
      resultMessage, resultDetails (JSONB)
    - Return new row id (untuk client traceability)

  REWRITE verifyDocument(POST /api/documents/:id/verify)
    - Method 1: QR payload + signature (HMAC-SHA256 verify)
    - Method 2: Manual hash compare ke doc.contentHash
    - Method 3: File upload — SHA-256 exact OR SimHash fuzzy
      (jika body.mode === 'hardcopy_simhash')
    - Response sekarang include: document (enriched), verificationRequestId
    - Insert verificationRequests row sebelum response

  REWRITE verifyByShortCode(GET /api/documents/verify?code=)
    - Insert verificationRequests dengan inputMethod='qr_scan'
    - Response include enriched document (+ blockchainAnchor)
    - Tetap public (tidak butuh auth)
    - Same-line tenant-lint-ignore comment dipakai untuk lookup by docId
      yang berasal dari HMAC-signed source

  BARU verifyByHash(POST /api/documents/verify-by-hash)
    - Body { hash } 64-char hex
    - findByContentHash (org-scoped jika authenticated)
    - Insert verificationRequests + return enriched doc
    - Public/optionalAuth — hash itself adalah credential

  BARU verifyByQrImage(POST /api/documents/verify-qr-image)
    - multipart/form-data file (PNG/JPG)
    - decodeQrFromBuffer → extract shortCode
    - Verify HMAC signature via qrcode.service.verifyQRPayload
    - Lookup doc by shortCode, insert verificationRequests
    - Response include signatureValid (true/false/null)

  BARU verifyByHardcopy(POST /api/documents/verify-hardcopy)
    - multipart/form-data file (PDF/DOCX/TXT)
    - computeUploadDNA → sha256 + simhash
    - First try SHA-256 exact match (= "verified" status, similarity 100%)
    - If no exact match dan hasText: findBySimHash → best match
    - Status: 'verified' (dist ≤ 6) | 'partial' (≤ 14) | 'not_found'
    - Return similarity %, distance, uploadedHash, uploadedSimHash

  BARU getDocumentQrImage(GET /api/documents/:id/qr-image)
    - Query documentQrCodes active terbaru untuk doc
    - s3Download dari uploadConfig.s3.qrBucket
    - Content-Type: image/png
    - Cache-Control: private, max-age=300

  ENRICH getDocument(GET /api/documents/:id)
    - Response data ditambah:
      qrCode: { shortCode, verificationUrl, hasQrImage, scanCount,
                lastScannedAt, createdAt } | null
      blockchainAnchor: { id, network, txHash, blockNumber, status,
                          anchoredAt } | null
    - Tidak break existing consumers (additive)

30.4 BACKEND ROUTES
--------------------

  src/routes/document.routes.js

  PUBLIC / optional-auth:
    POST /api/documents/verify-by-hash        verifyByHash
    POST /api/documents/verify-qr-image       verifyByQrImage    (multipart)
    POST /api/documents/verify-hardcopy       verifyByHardcopy   (multipart)
    GET  /api/documents/:id/qr-image          getDocumentQrImage

  EXISTING (rewritten internal):
    GET  /api/documents/verify?code=          verifyByShortCode
    POST /api/documents/:id/verify            verifyDocument (multipart)

30.5 FRONTEND
--------------

  Service: src/services/verification.service.js (NEW)
    - verifyByHash(hash)        POST /documents/verify-by-hash
    - verifyByShortCode(code)   GET  /documents/verify?code=
    - verifyByQrImage(file)     POST /documents/verify-qr-image (FormData)
    - verifyByHardcopy(file)    POST /documents/verify-hardcopy (FormData)
    - getQrImageUrl(documentId) GET  /documents/:id/qr-image (blob)
      Returns URL.createObjectURL(blob) — caller wajib revoke

  Komponen: src/features/documents/Verification.jsx (MAJOR REWRITE)
    - Hapus 100% Math.random() + setTimeout mock
    - Hapus animated QR scanner frame (camera mock)
    - State baru: errorMessage, hardFileMethod ∈ {qr_image, hardcopy}
    - handleSoftVerify: validate hash format → verifyByHash → render
      enriched document fields. 404 → set status=not_found tanpa toast
    - handleHardVerify: branch qr_image | hardcopy. Catch 404/422 ke
      not_found display, lain ke errorMessage banner
    - Tampil verificationRequestId di result panel (font-mono)
    - Hardcopy accept .pdf,.doc,.docx,.txt; QR image accept image/*

30.6 SIMHASH FUZZY ALGORITHM
-----------------------------

  Implementation: src/services/hash.service.js (EXISTING)
    - 64-bit fingerprint via FNV-1a hash + word shingles
    - hammingDistance(a, b) → 0..64

  Threshold mapping (verification.service.js SIMHASH_*):
    distance == 0       → 100% similarity, status verified
    distance ≤ 6        → ≥91% similarity, status verified
    distance 7..14      → 78..89% similarity, status partial
    distance > 14       → not_found (skip)

  Verified sanity test (smoke):
    "The quick brown fox jumps over the lazy dog."
    vs "The quick brown fox jumps over the lazy dog!!!"
    → distance 0 (punctuation stripped by normalizeText)

    vs "Completely unrelated text about cats and rainbows."
    → distance 36

  SSDEEP tetap placeholder ('PLACEHOLDER_SSDEEP') di hash.service.js.
  Tidak install ssdeep-nodejs (native compile beresiko di Alpine).
  ssdeepSimilarity column tetap null kecuali ada implementasi future.
  >>> OUTDATED (status saat audit 2026-06-30): pernyataan di atas adalah keadaan PER
  >>> 2026-05-24 dan SUDAH TIDAK BERLAKU. hash.service.js sekarang punya implementasi
  >>> spamsum pure-JS yang nyata (generateSSDeep → "blocksize:h1:h2"); tidak ada lagi
  >>> string 'PLACEHOLDER_SSDEEP'. ssdeepHash terisi untuk dokumen berteks dan
  >>> ssdeepSimilarity dihitung oleh ssdeepCompare(). Tetap homegrown (tidak kompatibel
  >>> ssdeep standar) & hanya tie-breaker — lihat §8.5 untuk deskripsi terkini.

30.7 QR DECODE PIPELINE
------------------------

  Dep baru backend:
    jsqr ^1.4.0  (pure JS QR decoder, no native binding)

  Reuse:
    sharp ^0.34.5  (image → raw RGBA conversion)

  Flow:
    1. Multer memoryStorage capture upload buffer
    2. sharp(buffer).ensureAlpha().raw().toBuffer({resolveWithObject:true})
    3. new Uint8ClampedArray(data)
    4. jsQR(rgba, width, height)
    5. JSON.parse(result.data) → { url, sig, payload }
    6. Extract shortCode dari payload.sc atau URL search param 'code'
    7. Lookup documentQrCodes by shortCode → enrich → log → respond

  Round-trip test verified: generate QR via qrcode.service ↔ decode via
  verification.service round-trip shortCode match.

30.8 verificationRequests ROW SHAPE
------------------------------------

  inputMethod ∈ {qr_scan, upload_softcopy, upload_hardcopy, manual_hash}
  status      ∈ {pending, verified, failed, revoked, expired}
                (saat verify: hanya verified / failed yang dipakai)
  isHardcopyScan: true untuk method upload_hardcopy
  simHashDistance: integer 0..64 (hanya untuk hardcopy fuzzy)
  ssdeepSimilarity: integer 0..100 (diisi oleh ssdeepCompare; dulu NULL — lihat catatan
                    OUTDATED di §30.6. Homegrown spamsum, tie-breaker only)
  blockchainVerified: true jika anchoredHash === doc.contentHash
  blockchainAnchorId: FK ke blockchainAnchors (latest anchor doc)
  resultDetails (JSONB):
    qr_scan / qr_image  → { signatureValid, shortCode }
    upload_hardcopy     → { topMatches: [{id, similarity, distance}...] }
    upload_softcopy     → { methods: [...] }
  completedAt: set ke now() saat insert (verify selalu synchronous)

30.9 SECURITY NOTES
--------------------

  - GET /api/documents/verify-by-hash SUDAH DIHAPUS dari backend (§48). Catatan
    lama di sini menyebutnya PUBLIC — rute itu tidak ada lagi. verification.service.js
    di frontend masih punya verifyByHash() yang memanggilnya; itu kode mati.
  - SEMUA endpoint verifikasi sekarang OWNER-ONLY (§66.C), termasuk QR image.
    Catatan lama "optional-auth" tidak berlaku lagi.
  - decodeQrFromBuffer tidak validate signature — itu tugas controller.
  - SSRF guard tetap dari upload-pipeline (sudah ada).

30.10 KNOWN LIMITATIONS
------------------------

  - Hardcopy fuzzy match butuh document text-extractable (PDF dengan teks,
    DOCX, TXT). Scan PDF image-only akan return 422 "Cannot extract text".
    Workaround: jalankan OCR upstream sebelum verify.
  - SimHash sensitive ke struktur kalimat. Edit besar (paragraf ditambah)
    bisa naikkan distance > 14 → not_found. Bukan bug — semantically itu
    dokumen berbeda dari sisi content fingerprint.
  - QR camera scan TIDAK didukung. User wajib upload image QR (PNG/JPG).
    Future option: install html5-qrcode di frontend untuk webcam capture.
  - SSDEEP column tetap mock — DocLoq schema docs (DATABASE_SCHEMA.md)
    masih mention ssdeepSimilarity tapi runtime tidak populate.

================================================================================
31. AI PROJECTS v1.2 — Strict source extraction + org usage (2026-05-24)
================================================================================

Update incremental untuk fitur AI Projects (Section 28). Menutup edge case
silent extraction failure + tambah analytics token usage per org.

  NOTE (2026-07-18): Sections 28 and 31 describe the v1.1/v1.2 state. The CURRENT
  AI Projects behavior — Studio outputs, AI image generation, the text/YouTube
  source types, citation-to-source popover, and the removed side tabs — is
  documented in §62.

31.1 STRICT SOURCE EXTRACTION
------------------------------

  Service: src/services/ai-project.service.js

  Sebelumnya: extractDocumentTextCache() return null silent kalau
  dekripsi/extract gagal atau hasil teks kosong. User akhirnya add source
  tapi chat tidak punya context — confusing.

  Sekarang: throw SourceExtractionError dengan code SOURCE_EXTRACTION_FAILED
  pada 4 kasus:
    1. Versi dokumen tidak ditemukan
    2. Dekripsi gagal (cipher, key tidak match)
    3. extractText throw (mime tidak didukung, parser error)
    4. Hasil teks <20 char ATAU >95% whitespace
       (function looksUsable check)

  Controller (ai-project.controller.js) mapError tangani error baru:
    SourceExtractionError    → HTTP 422 + code 'SOURCE_EXTRACTION_FAILED'
    SourcesEmptyError        → HTTP 422 + code 'SOURCES_EMPTY' + sources[]

  Frontend: AddDocumentModal + SourcesPanel sekarang surface error message
  ke user (toast/badge), tidak silent.

  Status `aiProjectSources.status` baru: 'failed' (selain active/removed).
  Re-add source dengan status 'failed' akan trigger re-extract.

31.2 ORG TOKEN USAGE ANALYTICS
-------------------------------

  Endpoint baru:
    GET /api/ai-projects/usage?days=30

  Auth: authenticate (any user di org). Tidak butuh admin role.

  Service: getOrgUsage(orgId, rangeDays=30) di ai-project.service.js
    - SQL aggregate per-day dari aiProjectChats.metadata JSONB:
      promptTokens, completionTokens (di-cast ::int dari JSON)
    - Group by DATE(createdAt)
    - Filter role='assistant' untuk counter responses
    - INNER JOIN aiProjects WHERE organizationId = orgId

  Response shape:
    {
      success: true,
      data: {
        rangeDays: 30,
        daily: [
          { date: '2026-05-23', promptTokens, completionTokens,
            totalTokens, responses }, ...
        ],
        totals: { promptTokens, completionTokens, totalTokens, responses }
      }
    }

  Max range: 365 days (clamped).

31.3 CACHE LAYER REFINEMENTS
-----------------------------

  Service: src/services/ai-cache.service.js
    - Tighter TTL handling untuk cache hit re-validation
    - Prevent stale cache saat source list berubah

  Service: src/services/ai-analysis.service.js
    - Minor cleanup di extract path (consistent error messages)

31.4 NEW ERROR CODES (API contract)
------------------------------------

  HTTP 422 | code: SOURCE_EXTRACTION_FAILED
    Source tidak bisa diparse. Message detail human-readable.
    Body: { success:false, code, message, sourceId }

  HTTP 422 | code: SOURCES_EMPTY
    Chat sent tapi semua source di project sudah dalam state failed/empty.
    Body: { success:false, code, message, sources: [...] }

31.5 NO DB MIGRATION
---------------------

  Semua field sudah ada di schema 0012_ai_projects.sql. Update ini hanya
  semantic / behavior change. Backward compatible.

32. OCR PIPELINE UPGRADE + EXTENSION EXPANSION (2026-05-29)
================================================================================

Scope: scanned PDFs + images become first-class content (AI Analysis, AI Projects,
upload), file-type support widened, OCR accuracy pipeline hardened. All self-hosted.

32.1 OCR ENGINE & PDF RENDER
----------------------------

  - Engine: tesseract.js (WASM, in-process, no separate OCR server). RAM ~250MB
    with eng+ind+osd workers loaded; workers auto-terminate after 5 min idle.
  - PDF text + page→image render unified on pdfjs (single version) — avoids the
    "API version does not match Worker version" clash from pdf-parse's bundled pdfjs.
    pdf-parse v2 (class API) is no longer used in the OCR/extract path.
  - PDF page render: pdfjs + @napi-rs/canvas (sharp build lacks poppler/pdfium).
  - Language: eng+ind (configurable OCR_LANG). traineddata self-hosted in
    vendor/tessdata/ (eng, ind, osd) — no CDN fetch at runtime (UU PDP).

32.2 ACCURACY PIPELINE (processImageForAI)
------------------------------------------

  EXIF auto-rotate → Tesseract OSD orientation (0/90/180/270, Legacy engine) →
  enhance (grayscale → normalize → CLAHE adaptive → sharpen → upscale) →
  OCR (PSM 6 single-block + user_defined_dpi) →
  if confidence < OCR_MIN_CONFIDENCE: retry once with aggressive enhance
    (median denoise + binarize) and keep the higher-confidence result →
  cleanOcrText (join hyphenation, drop noise lines, normalize whitespace).

  - OSD bug fixed: orientation_degrees applied directly as clockwise rotation
    (earlier 360-deg formula mis-rotated 90° pages).
  - OCR_RENDER_DPI default 300 (measured: 400/500 reduced accuracy here because of
    interaction with the enhance upscale step — higher DPI dropped page titles).
  - tessdata_best (full LSTM) was evaluated for >95% accuracy but CRASHES the
    tesseract.js v7 WASM core (missing function DotProductSSE) — NOT shipped.

32.3 FILE-TYPE EXPANSION (single source of truth)
-------------------------------------------------

  upload.config.js now holds the canonical allowlist + category map + blocklist,
  shared by: multer fileFilter (document.routes.js), the upload pipeline validator,
  and AI Project source ingestion. Categories: pdf | office | spreadsheet |
  presentation | image | text.

  Added formats (upload AND AI Project, identical):
    ODT, RTF, ODS, ODP, WebP, TIFF, BMP, CSV, Markdown
  Extraction libs: mammoth (DOCX), SheetJS/xlsx (spreadsheets), jszip (PPTX/ODT/ODP),
  internal RTF stripper, pdfjs (PDF), tesseract.js (images/scanned PDF).

  Dangerous-file blocklist (defense-in-depth, on top of magic-byte + ClamAV):
    Ext  : exe bat cmd com scr msi msp dll sys drv sh bash zsh ps1 psm1 vbs vbe
           wsf wsh hta js mjs cjs jse jar apk app deb rpm dmg pkg run bin gadget
           cpl lnk reg pif
    MIME : application/x-msdownload, x-executable, x-dosexec, x-sh, x-shellscript,
           x-msi, vnd.microsoft.portable-executable, x-elf, java-archive, ...
  Helpers: isBlockedFile(name, mime), isAllowedUpload(mime, name).

32.4 AI PROJECT SOURCE = ASYNC
------------------------------

  Document sources no longer block the request during extraction/OCR (a multi-page
  scanned PDF can take many seconds). addDocumentSource inserts status='processing'
  and returns immediately; background processDocumentIngest sets 'active'+contentText
  or 'failed'+error_message. retryDocumentSource re-runs a failed/removed source.
  Unified retry endpoint dispatches by source type (document vs url).

32.5 BUGFIX (pre-existing)
--------------------------

  document.controller.js imported the non-existent text-extraction.service.js →
  re-pointed to upload-pipeline.service.js (extractText). PDF text extraction was
  silently failing (pdf-parse v2 API) and falling back to raw-byte hashing.

32.6 ENV (new)
--------------

  OCR_RENDER_DPI=300        PDF render DPI before OCR
  OCR_MIN_CONFIDENCE=55     aggressive-retry threshold (0-100)
  OCR_ENABLE_OSD=true       orientation auto-correct (needs osd.traineddata)

32.7 DEPLOYMENT NOTES
---------------------

  - vendor/tessdata/ (eng+ind+osd, ~16MB) must be present at runtime. Dockerfile
    now COPY vendor/ and apk add fontconfig freetype font-noto (for @napi-rs/canvas).
    Verified rendering on alpine musl.
  - No DB migration: ai_project_sources.status already supports 'processing'.
  - Tests: node:test suite (npm test) — 22 cases (config/blocklist/extract/OCR/OSD).

33. FUNCTIONAL AUDIT FIXES (2026-05-30)
================================================================================

A static code audit of all 112 functional-test scenarios (12 sections) found 29 items
that were not truly "Pass" (7 Fail, 7 Gap, 15 Partial). The following fixes were applied,
ordered by severity. (P3 doc-alignment items are tracked separately, not code changes.)

--- P0: CRITICAL (security / crash) ---

P0.1 QR verification crash — FIXED
  backend/src/controllers/document.controller.js
  verifyByShortCode / verifyByQrImage called resolveQrVerification() which was never
  defined → ReferenceError, whole QR-verify flow dead. Implemented resolveQrVerification(qr, doc):
  returns verdict VALID / SUPERSEDED / REVOKED / PURGED / NOT_FOUND with currentShortCode +
  currentVersionNumber, by comparing scanned QR version vs the document's current active QR.

P0.2 Download endpoints lacked permission checks — FIXED
  backend/src/controllers/document.controller.js
  downloadDocumentDecrypted + downloadDocumentConverted only checked org membership — any
  authenticated user in the org could download/convert ANY document, including soft-deleted ones.
  Added assertDocumentAccess(req, doc) helper (mirrors getDocument: owner / getUserPermissionLevel
  / active task → else 403) + isNull(deletedAt) filter (soft-deleted → 404). Applied to both handlers.

P0.3 Team permissions + subfolder inheritance not enforced — FIXED
  backend/src/middlewares/permission.middleware.js
  getUserPermissionLevel only matched folderPermissions.userId — team grants and
  inheritToSubfolders were stored but never honored. Now: resolves the user's teamMembers and
  matches OR(userId, teamId IN teams) on both document & folder permissions; walks the folder
  ancestor chain (parentId) and honors inheritToSubfolders on ancestor grants. Highest level wins.

--- P1: FUNCTIONAL ---

P1.1 Task notifications not sent — FIXED
  task.controller.js: createTask now calls createNotification(assignee) on assignment.
  signing.controller.js: processSigningDecline now notifies task.createdBy on decline.
  (Overdue notifications still require a scheduler — see Deferred below.)

P1.2 Delete user did not clear downloadWatermarks — FIXED
  user.controller.js: deleteUser now UPDATE downloadWatermarks SET downloadedBy=null before
  deleting the user (forensic records preserved, not reliant on DB FK alone).

P1.3 Folder soft-delete orphaned documents — FIXED
  folder.controller.js: deleteFolder now soft-deletes contained documents AND inserts trashItems
  rows (30-day autoDeleteAt), instead of nulling folderId (orphan to root). Mirrors deleteDocument.

P1.4 Chatbot null-org crash — FIXED
  chatbot.controller.js: sendMessage early-returns 400 with a clear message when
  user.organizationId is missing, before processMessage (was a 500 bubble).

--- P2: AUDIT / ROBUSTNESS ---

P2.1 securityEvents never written — FIXED
  auth.controller.js: inserts securityEvents (type 'brute_force', severity high) on account
  lockout (5 failed attempts). upload-pipeline.service.js: inserts securityEvents
  (type 'malware_detected', severity critical) when ClamAV flags a file.

P2.2 Vault failure had no fallback — FIXED
  encryption.service.js: generateDocumentKey now try/catches the Vault provider and falls back to
  the local key provider (flag vaultUnavailable). upload-pipeline pushes a warning when fallback used.
  NOTE: fallback uses a local key — acceptable for availability; review for high-security tenants.

P2.3 Empty 0-byte files not rejected — FIXED
  upload-pipeline.service.js: Step 1 now rejects file.size===0 / buffer.length===0
  ("File kosong tidak diizinkan").

P2.4 Trash countdown — PARTIAL (countdown done; shred/auto-purge deferred)
  trash.controller.js: listTrash now returns daysRemaining (from autoDeleteAt) per item for UI.
  DEFERRED (need infra/scheduler): cryptographic shredding on permanent delete (cryptoShredding
  table unused) + background auto-purge of expired trash + proactive overdue-task notifications.
  These require a backend scheduler (node-cron / setInterval) — tracked as a follow-up.

--- P3: DOC ALIGNMENT (no code change, by-design) ---
  - Login is 2FA-mandatory for email/password (auth.controller.js); Google OAuth skips 2FA
    (Google provides its own) — by design, not a bug. Update test table wording.
  - AI chat retrieves a token-budgeted set across all active sources (not a fixed top-8).
  - Analysis/source truncation is 30K/32K chars (not 15K).
  - Watermark scanner returns found+confidence (no explicit amber/gray color field).

All changed files pass `node --check`. Migration note: no new tables; existing securityEvents,
trashItems, downloadWatermarks, teamMembers, folderPermissions columns are reused.
